### Consensus Summary — cycle 3

**Agreed strengths.** The same private class-family predicate governs restore-marker emission and receiving dispatch cleanup. `ListView` and `CardListView` inherit Django generic ListView and set `cv_object=False` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/list.py:19,26`; `src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17,24`); filtered compositions retain that family (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:299`; `tests/test1/app/views.py:299,367`). Guardian already uses `MultipleObjectMixin` as a list-family test (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:246`). Forms, selector and manage views remain outside the cleanup family. This is restoration classification, not an authorization grant or automatic-origin expansion.

**R22 closure.** Plan 01:100 explicitly defines `is_cancel_list_destination` and separates family membership from origin eligibility; 01:105/107 uses it on the accepted resolved origin and receiving class. Task 01-03:144–146 follows a rendered link from an explicitly allowlisted custom-key filtered list, seeds older destination session state after capture, follows Cancel, asserts unfiltered records and cleared remembered state, checks a later bare visit and cleaned controls/action captures, repeats with persistence disabled, and verifies auto rejects the same custom origin. The automated failure statement names these regressions. All three reviewers expressly sign off full plan-level resolution. Existing permission, success and selector contracts in plans 02/03 remain coherent.

## Claude Review (fable-review) — cycle 3

# Cycle 3 review of 2defe83

Scope: read-only review of the five committed plans at 2defe83 against the current source checkout. The diff from the cycle 2 baseline touches only 06.7-01-PLAN.md and 06.7-VALIDATION.md, both additive. No HIGH or MEDIUM finding remains. R22 is signed off at plan level below.

## 06.7-01

**Summary.** Wave 1 delivers the signed transport, the URL resolver, automatic form defaults, shared list-family dispatch cleanup and explicit-empty restoration. The R22 revision adds one private predicate that both the emitter and the receiving list use, plus a custom-key test contract in 01-03.

**Strengths.**
- **The family predicate matches every real list composition.** The core list is `ListView(CrudView, generic.ListView)` with `cv_object = False` at `src/crud_views/lib/views/list.py:19` and `:26`. The card list is `CardListView(CardOrderMixin, CrudView, generic.ListView)` with `cv_object = False` at `src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17` and `:24`. Filtered compositions mix in `ListViewTableFilterMixin(FilterView)` at `src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:299`, and the installed django-filter base is `BaseFilterView(FilterMixin, MultipleObjectMixin, View)` at `django_filters/views.py:71`. The test app's filtered list and filtered cards at `tests/test1/app/views.py:299`, `:367` and `:385` therefore all satisfy "MultipleObjectMixin subclass with cv_object False" regardless of key.
- **The predicate excludes exactly the no-object views that must keep their navigation fields.** `ManageView` is a `TemplateView` at `src/crud_views/lib/views/manage.py:10`. `CreateView` at `create.py:17`, `CustomFormNoObjectView` at `form.py:48` and `PolymorphicCreateSelectView` at `src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:24` are form views with `cv_object = False` and no `MultipleObjectMixin`. So forms and the selector retain origin and token through invalid POST, as 01 requires, and the cleanup never touches them.
- **Guardian lists fall into the same family without special casing.** `GuardianListViewPermissionRequired` and `GuardianCardListViewPermissionRequired` compose the core list classes at `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:245` and `:251`. The Guardian package already uses the identical `issubclass(cls, MultipleObjectMixin)` test for composition validation at `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:246`, so the predicate follows an established project idiom.
- **Custom registration keys are real and session-scoped, so the R22 test is meaningful.** `register_view_class` accepts any key at `src/crud_views/lib/viewset/__init__.py:318`, the key regex at `src/crud_views/lib/check.py:14` admits `archive`, and `SessionData.view_key` is `viewset.name-cv_key` at `src/crud_views/lib/session.py:44`. An `archive` list therefore has its own `filter_query_string` slot, and the bare-request redirect at `mixins.py:395` is exactly the stale-filter hazard the test seeds and must defeat.
- **Cleanup at dispatch precedes every control that could leak a navigation field.** `CrudView.dispatch` at `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:227` runs before `View.dispatch` reaches `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get`. Pagination copies `request.GET` at `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:559`, card order controls read it at `mixins.py:273`, filter hidden inputs read it at `list.py:114`, and the JS config reads `QUERY_STRING` at `crud_views.py:109`. Cleaning `request.GET` and `META` once therefore satisfies the "absent from hidden inputs and control URLs" assertion by construction.
- **Eligibility stays separate from restoration.** The interface states that family membership grants no origin eligibility and that auto remains registered list/card/detail. The 01-03 acceptance tests the negative case explicitly: a registered custom list under auto configuration is neither selected nor captured and Cancel uses the fallback.

**Concerns.**
- **LOW, execution note, not a plan defect.** Required property: the custom-key route is reachable in the request tests. `ViewSet.urlpatterns` is a `cached_property` at `src/crud_views/lib/viewset/__init__.py:403`, so a list registered on an existing ViewSet after the root URLconf materialized is unreachable. The repository already has the right pattern: fresh ViewSets with a temporary `ROOT_URLCONF` at `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py:26` through `:207`. No plan text change is needed because the task already says "register a filtered ListView under a distinct key", and a wrong fixture fails loudly with a 404.
- **Cosmetic.** The 01-01 action still says "consume and clean list/card navigation". The interface block governs and 01-01 implements "the interfaces above", so this is wording drift only.

**Risk.** Low. The remaining risk is the known atomic-tracer size, already justified under R5.

## 06.7-02

**Summary.** Unchanged since cycle 2. It adds E256, the runtime `ImproperlyConfigured` fallback diagnostic, the registry inventory and the Guardian target adapters.

**Strengths.**
- **E256 is still free.** A source search finds no `E256` declaration under `src`, and `E255` remains the context-button check at `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392`. R1 closure holds.
- **R22 does not disturb the permission contract.** Target availability dispatches on mixin type, not key. A custom-key list under Guardian hits the queryset allow-entry policy at `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:129`, and the parent check at `:268` through `:277` runs before `CrudView.dispatch`, so cleanup never precedes or influences authorization.
- **E256 is defined over effective allowed origins, which already includes explicit custom keys.** No wording change was needed for R22.

**Concerns.** None.

**Risk.** Low. The R9 serialized base.py ownership remains the only coordination hazard in wave 2, and it is explicit.

A concrete create that allows an explicit custom list key resolves it through the same `read_cancel_state` and predicate path, so the custom-key case needs no selector-specific branch.
- **Success stays key-only.** `cv_get_success_key` at `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:774` is untouched and the selector keeps dropping `cv_from`, so R18/R19 remain intact.

**Concerns.** None.

**Risk.** Low. The tag is a thin adapter over the plan 01 resolver and the matrix is explicit.

Stating in one sentence that any explicitly allowed list or card destination restores captured state is within that existing scope. No revision is required.

## OpenCode Review (grok-review) — cycle 3

R22 is a class-family claim. I’ll check List, Card, and filter view MROs against that predicate before signing it off.
## 06.7-01

**Summary.** R22 is closed at plan level. `is_cancel_list_destination` is a class-family test, not a key test, and 01-03 makes the custom-key empty-restore case fail if that predicate is skipped. Automatic eligibility stays list/card/detail. **R22: FULLY RESOLVED.**

**Strengths.**
- `ListView` is `generic.ListView` with `cv_key = "list"` and `cv_object = False` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/list.py:19-26`). Django’s `ListView` is `MultipleObjectTemplateResponseMixin, BaseListView`, and `BaseListView` is `MultipleObjectMixin, View` (installed `django/views/generic/list.py:150-151,220`). A renamed subclass stays in the family.
- `CardListView` is the same list base with `cv_key = "card"` and `cv_object = False` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17-24`). Filtered cards compose the filter mixin in front of that class (`tests/test1/app/views.py:367`).
- `ListViewTableFilterMixin` is `FilterView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:299`). Installed `FilterView` goes through `BaseFilterView(FilterMixin, MultipleObjectMixin, View)` (`django_filters/views.py:71-91`). `PublisherListView` is that mixin plus `ListViewPermissionRequired` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:299`). The predicate matches all three compositions without reading `cv_key`.
- `ManageView` is `generic.TemplateView` with `cv_object = False` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/manage.py:10-16`). Create and no-object forms set `cv_object = False` but are not list views (`src/crud_views/lib/views/create.py:17`, `form.py:48`). The predicate does not pull them in.
- Registry accepts any key (`src/crud_views/lib/viewset/__init__.py:318-320`). Empty query still revives stored filters only when persistence sees a bare request (`mixins.py:395-397`). Session identity is `{viewset}-{cv_key}` (`src/crud_views/lib/session.py:42-44`), so an `archive` seed is a different bucket from `list`. 01-03 requires a rendered link, `restore=1`, unfiltered return, cleared `filter_query_string`, a later bare visit, cleaned controls, disabled-persistence no-write, and auto rejection of that key.
- The interface says family membership grants no origin eligibility. Auto remains registered list/card/detail. Success is still excluded from captured query. Plan 02’s access adapter and plan 03’s selector handoff are unchanged.

**Concerns.**
- **LOW.** `ViewSet.urlpatterns` is a cached property built once (`viewset/__init__.py:403-434`) and already consumed at `tests/test1/app/urls.py:40`. Late registration on an imported ViewSet will not be reversible unless the test installs a fresh pattern list. This does not weaken the predicate. It is how the required request test has to be wired.

**Suggestions.**
- Build the archive case on a test-local ViewSet and assign `module.urlpatterns` the way `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py:165` does. Seed `SessionData` from that archive view, not from `list`.

**Risk Assessment: LOW.** The required property is executable and the predicate matches the real list, card, and filter MROs.

- Nested Guardian create still denies `obj is None` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:381-382`). 02-02 still branches parent-only targets away from that call. A custom-key list is a queryset/list target, not a new permission rule.

## 06.7-05

**Strengths.**
- Final gates still run the package suite that will include `test_cancel_state.py`, and still refuse golden regeneration.

## Cross-plan

R22 does not change origin eligibility, success handling, selector forwarding, or permission. Auto still admits only registered list/card/detail. An explicit `cv_cancel_keys` entry can name a filtered list whose `cv_key` is not `list`. Restore emission and GET cleanup both use `MultipleObjectMixin` plus `cv_object=False`, which is true for stock list, card, and filter compositions and false for manage, create, and selector forms. ## OpenCode Review (gemini-review) — cycle 3

## 06.7-01

### Summary
Revised Plan `06.7-01` (`2defe83`) introduces `is_cancel_list_destination(cls)` in `src/crud_views/lib/view/cancel.py`—checking `issubclass(cls, django.views.generic.list.MultipleObjectMixin) and cls.cv_object is False`—and uses that single class-family predicate for both restore-marker (`cv_from_restore=1`) emission in `CrudView.cv_get_cancel_url()` and request cleanup in `CrudView._cv_consume_cancel_restore()` (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:44,99,104,106`), while keeping `effective_cancel_keys()` for `"auto"` restricted to registered `"list"`, `"card"`, and (when `cv_object=True`) `"detail"`. Task `01-03` (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:144-145`) adds an end-to-end test in `tests/test1/test_cancel_state.py` registering a filtered `ListView` under a custom key (`"archive"`), verifying empty-state restoration against seeded `SessionData`, clean filter/order/pagination controls, disabled-persistence non-mutation, and non-selection under `"auto"`. **R22 is FULLY RESOLVED at the plan level**, and prior HIGH closures **R2**, **R3**, and **R4** remain intact.

### Strengths
- **Exact class-family predicate matching all package list/card/filter compositions (`R22`)**:
  - `ListView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/list.py:19,26`) and `CardListView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17,24`) both subclass `django.views.generic.ListView` (which inherits `MultipleObjectMixin`) and declare `cv_object = False`.
  - `ListViewTableFilterMixin` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:299`) subclasses `django_filters.views.FilterView` (which also inherits `MultipleObjectMixin`), and `ResourceViewMixin` list views (`src/crud_views/lib/resource.py:140`) subclass `ListViewPermissionRequired`.
  - Using `MultipleObjectMixin` with `cv_object=False` matches the package's existing list-view class check in `GuardianParentPermissionMixin._guardian_child_composition_error()` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:246`), while excluding all non-list views (`DetailView` at `src/crud_views/lib/views/detail.py:7`, `CreateView` at `create.py:12`, `UpdateView` at `update.py:9`, `DeleteView` at `delete.py:51`, `CustomFormView` / `CustomFormNoObjectView` at `form.py:10,40`, `PolymorphicCreateSelectView` at `src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:24`, `ActionView` at `action.py:12`, and `ManageView` at `manage.py:10`).
- **Orthogonality between destination-family restoration and origin allowlisting (`D-02`, `D-03`, `R22`)**: `is_cancel_list_destination(cls)` is evaluated only after an origin key has been accepted by `effective_cancel_keys(owner)` (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:99,104`), so `"auto"` still admits only registered `"list"`/`"card"`/`"detail"` keys while an explicitly allowlisted custom key (e.g. `cv_cancel_keys = ["archive"]`) gets full `cv_from_restore=1` emission, `SessionData` (`src/crud_views/lib/session.py:43-45`, scoped by `f"{self.view.cv_viewset.name}-{self.view.cv_key}"`) empty-filter clearing (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:376-377,395-397`), and dispatch-level query cleaning.
- **Preserved Wave 1 closures (`R2`, `R3`, `R4`)**: Shared dispatch cleanup (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:106`), narrow golden normalization after asserting emitted transport (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:129`), and Wave 1 migration of the 12 affected regression modules (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:15-26,129-130`) remain unchanged.

### Concerns
- None.

### Suggestions
- None.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW** — `R22` and all prior Wave 1 findings (`R2`, `R3`, `R4`, `R5`, `R6`, `R11`, `R15`, `R17`, `R18`, `R19`) are fully specified and covered by automated verification in Tasks `01-01`, `01-02`, and `01-03`.

## 06.7-02

### Summary
Plan `06.7-02` (`2defe83`, unchanged from `dfd98c2`) defines the unique `viewset.E256` startup check and runtime `ImproperlyConfigured` fallback diagnostic in `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py` and implements Guardian's `_cv_cancel_target_available(cls, user, obj, rendering_view)` in `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py`. Its consumption of Plan `06.7-01`'s interfaces remains coherent with the `R22` revision, and prior HIGH closures **R1**, **R4**, and **R16** remain valid.

### Strengths
- **Unique `viewset.E256` check and full registry inventory (`R1`, `R8`)**: `06.7-02-PLAN.md:62-64` keeps `viewset.E255` on `context_button_checks` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392`), proves `E256` uniqueness across `src/`, and audits all registered cancel-owning forms in `test_registered_cancel_fallback_inventory`.
- **Target-owned Guardian parent, object, queryset, and parent-only/create branches (`R16`, `R17`)**: `06.7-02-PLAN.md:71-72` enforces active/authenticated actor checks (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:14-21,46,119,270`), validates child mixin composition (`mixins.py:235`), checks `_guardian_user_has_parent_perm` (`mixins.py:303`) and `cv_create_has_access` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:310`) without falling through to model-level `cv_has_access(user, None)` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:926-930`), and evaluates `_cv_action_enabled_for_rendering` (`base.py:377`) once.

### Concerns
- None.

### Suggestions
- None.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW** — Coherent with Plan `06.7-01`; all prior findings (`R1`, `R4`, `R8`, `R9`, `R15`, `R16`, `R17`) remain closed.

## 06.7-03

### Summary
Plan `06.7-03` (`2defe83`, unchanged from `dfd98c2`) forwards validated cancel state through `PolymorphicCreateSelectView.form_valid()` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:68-72`) and adds `{% cv_cancel_url %}` to `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py`. The `R22` addition of `is_cancel_list_destination(cls)` in `src/crud_views/lib/view/cancel.py` seamlessly supports custom-key list origins forwarded through the selector when explicitly allowlisted on the concrete `PolymorphicCreateView` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create.py:13`), while keeping `"auto"` and `cv_success_keys` unchanged.

### Strengths
- **Token-only selector forwarding and 4-axis configuration matrix (`R18`, `R19`)**: `06.7-03-PLAN.md:63-64` forwards only the bounded `cv_from_cancel` token (or `"invalid"` marker on rejected input, or `encode_cancel_state(view, origin, None)` for validated legacy input) without forwarding `cv_from`, preserving existing concrete-create save redirects (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:68-72`).
- **Unified rendering contract and custom `FormActions` helper coverage (`R14`, `R20`)**: `06.7-03-PLAN.md:73` normalizes empty/unresolved `obj` arguments in `cv_cancel_url` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:284`) and tests custom `get_form_actions()` and `CrispyDeleteForm` helper subclasses filtering `None` (`src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py:57-58,138-149`).

### Concerns
- None.

### Suggestions
- None.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW** — Disjoint Wave 2 file ownership and serialized `base.py` defect handling (`R9`) remain intact.

### R22 — MEDIUM — custom-key filtered-list restoration

**Required property:** Every permitted filtered-list origin, including one registered under a custom key, preserves explicit-empty captured state and consumes internal navigation fields before persistence or subsequent controls.

**Evidence:** `.planning/phases/06.7-dynamic-cancel-navigation-and-origin-query-state/06.7-01-PLAN.md:103` says the restore marker applies to “accepted list/card captures”; line 105 likewise limits cleanup to “GET list/card destinations.” It does not define whether this means destination family or literal registered keys. The custom-key tests in 01-02 and ordinary destination matrix in 01-03 do not combine custom-key filtering with an empty capture. `src/crud_views/lib/views/list.py:24` makes the key a class attribute; `src/crud_views/lib/viewset/__init__.py:318` accepts a key when registering a class; `src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:395` restores stored query on a bare request. A literal-key implementation could pass the listed standard-key cases yet restore stale filters for an accepted custom-key list. This is the new edge Fable identified, not reopening R2's fixed ordinary-card consumer.

**Example remedy (non-binding):** Define shared list-family restoration eligibility independent of registration key and test custom-key filtered-list empty restoration with old session state, plus clean follow-on controls. Other mechanisms meeting the property are equally valid.

### Divergent views and non-actionable execution notes

- **Local assets (Fable MEDIUM versus Grok/Gemini accepted prerequisite): already incorporated, not a new revision requirement.** Plan 05:58/62 explicitly declares the external local asset prerequisite, prohibits downloads/CDN fallback, tests absent/invalid assets, and requires unverified evidence when absent. Plan 05:72 fails completion when required browser cases are skipped. “Unverified prerequisite” and “completion gate fails” are compatible. Prior R21 explicitly allowed a declared prerequisite. No provisioning script or changed network policy is required to meet that property; absence must never be reported as passing browser acceptance.
- **Grok GET-cache claim: rejected on source evidence.** Installed Django `django/core/handlers/wsgi.py:85` uses `@cached_property`; `django/utils/functional.py:7-48` defines a non-data descriptor with `__get__` and no `__set__`. Assigning `request.GET` is valid and replaces the cached entry. Plan 01:105 already requires cleaning both GET and META, with assertions at 01-03. Direct `__dict__` manipulation is not an additional requirement.
- **Gemini synthetic dict request:** the existing fixture at `tests/test1/test_core_nested_create_state.py:103` uses `GET={}`. Plan 01-02 already owns this exact module's fixture migration and runs the whole file before wave 2. Updating a synthetic fixture to a realistic QueryDict is an admissible route; expanding production acceptance to arbitrary dict-backed requests is not required. The current executable gate exposes this incompatibility, so it is not invisible work stranded in this report.
- **Gemini token-only key-helper suggestion:** token-only URL resolution is explicit in 01:100/101/103 and 03-01's matrix; success stays key-only. The old `cv_get_cancel_key` is deliberately retained as a key helper and explicitly removed from the supported cancel integration contract (01:107; D-13). Requiring that legacy helper to acquire the full new transport contract is not a missing phase obligation.
- **Gemini top-level inactive actors:** 02-02 explicitly denies inactive users even with forced sessions, checks anonymous entry before its nested-target branches, and retains policy tests. This covers the unconditional actor guard; no new scope is missing.
- **Gemini parser/concrete allow-list note:** 01 specifies envelope validation; 03-01 explicitly applies the concrete class's allowed origins and tests independent auto/static and explicit-list combinations. Those tests already fail an incorrectly selector-scoped parser. This is execution guidance for existing acceptance criteria, not a new finding.
- **Example Delete/Update consistency (Fable/Gemini):** 04-03 already owns both files, requires replacing the feature explanation and inventories current cancel claims while preserving intentional explicit-list examples. Removing Delete's explicit list is one optional implementation; documenting it accurately also satisfies the task.
- Fable's optional per-request signing cache, detail-query wording, stronger startup detection of explicit bad fallbacks, selector fixture naming and companion rerun note are advisory. Existing tasks already document query restoration, deliberately choose lazy runtime fallback diagnostics, use actual Vehicle fixtures, and require accurate acceptance/runtime limits. No companion delivery is added to this phase. Grok's registry, legacy-modal and direct-success suggestions restate existing pre-inventory/legacy/preservation requirements.

# Review: Phase 06.7 plans (dynamic cancel navigation)

Verified against the working tree at `beea29c`. Existing `src/` is authoritative; I did not run the test suite, only read-only greps and file reads. One sandbox limit: I could not list `~/.cache/ms-playwright`, so the browser runtime availability claimed by RESEARCH.md is unverified here.

## 06.7-01

**Summary.** The plan correctly identifies every seam it must change: the static defaults at `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:81-82`, the key-only emitter at `base.py:808-815`, the user-ignoring cancel context at `base.py:817-824`, the bare-list restoration at `src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:391-397`, and the crispy action group at `src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py:57-58,138-149`. The interface block is unusually precise and the signed, bounded transport is a sound fit for the project's security enforcement. Two things are missing: the plan has no consumer for the restore marker on card pages, and it does not acknowledge that the new default changes four committed goldens and at least fourteen exact-link assertions in test files its verify commands never run.

**Strengths.**
- `cv_cancel_keys="auto"` on `CrudViewProcessFormMixin` lands on exactly the form family D-02 names: `create.py:12`, `update.py:9`, `delete.py:51`, `form.py:10,40` all put the mixin before `CrudView`, and `WorkflowView(CustomFormView)` at `src/crud_views_workflow/lib/views.py:18` inherits it. List, card, detail and action views stay at `None` (`base.py:82`).
- The existing F2 rule at `base.py:803-805` already prevents an object page from sending an origin to a no-object target, so D-04 (no detail → create capture) holds without new code.
- The plan calls out the two places where a string sentinel would be silently iterated: `base.py:523-525` would check `is_view_registered("a")` and fire E252, and `base.py:796` would splat `"auto"` into four one-character keys. Both are named in the interface block.
- `ViewSet.app` exists (`src/crud_views/lib/viewset/__init__.py:71`), so the proposed `viewset=[app or "", name]` binding is implementable as written.
- Restoring explicit-empty state before the stored-query redirect is placed correctly: `mixins.py:395-397` is the only branch that revives old filters, and `session.py:71-80` persists only on clean exit, which the plan relies on for "no write when disabled".
- Reserving three derived names from `origin_param` (`settings.py:38-39`) avoids a new setting and keeps `test_custom_origin_param_name` (`test_cancel_origin.py:369-379`) meaningful.

**Concerns.**
- **HIGH. Card origins have no restore-marker consumer, so the marker leaks and accumulates.** `CardListView(CardOrderMixin, CrudView, generic.ListView)` at `src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17` does not include `ListViewTableFilterMixin`, yet the interface says to append the restore scalar "for accepted list/card captures". On a card page the marker would survive in `request.GET`, be copied into the order form's hidden inputs by `mixins.py:271-279`, and into every pagination link by `templatetags/crud_views.py:557-569`. A later row action would then capture a query the plan promised to clean only "on capture", and the browser URL keeps the marker across order changes. The plan's only consumption point is `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get`.
- **HIGH. The default change alters committed goldens the plan does not mention.** `tests/test1/golden/action_menus/{list_full,toolbar_detail_full,cards_author_full,toolbar_publisher_list}.html` contain hrefs to update/delete/create. They are rendered from `cv_author` and `cv_publisher` (`test_action_menus_golden.py:88,106,118,132,142`), whose form views have no explicit keys (`test_cancel_origin.py:47-53` asserts `cv_cancel_keys is None` on `cv_author`'s update view). Under `auto`, those links gain `?cv_from=…&cv_from_cancel=<signed token>`. PROJECT.md's byte-identical rule allows exceptions only "where a phase says so", and neither ROADMAP nor this plan says so. The token is deterministic under the fixed `SECRET_KEY="django-testing"` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:46`), but a golden containing a signature tied to a salt and secret is brittle, and Plan 05 later forbids "blindly regenerating goldens". A decision is needed here, not in wave 4.
- **MEDIUM. Blast radius of the default is discovered too late.** Fourteen exact `href="{…create|update|delete…}"` assertions exist across seven files (`test_guardian_parent_none.py`, `test_authorization_templates.py`, `test_authorization_matrix.py`, `test_sibling_context_button.py`, `test_core_nested_create_state.py`, `test_inaccessible_actions_resolve.py`, `test_action_menus_groups.py`). At least the card-page ones break: `test_guardian_parent_none.py:875` asserts `row["cv_url"] == create_url` on a card page, and `card.py:77` routes that URL through `cv_get_link_url`, so a nested create (auto, `card` registered) now carries an origin. Plan 01's three verify commands run none of these files; the full suite first runs in Plan 05-02. Plan 02's verify then runs `test_guardian_parent_none.py` and will fail on a Plan 01 change it does not own.
- **MEDIUM. The "tracer" is not thin.** Task 01-01 touches five production files and asks for "the complete bounded parser at its creation" plus the core target adapter, the crispy omission path and list restoration. That is the whole architecture in one task, which defeats the tracer's purpose as an early integration checkpoint and makes the red-first test a poor diagnostic when it fails.
- **LOW. Token bound exceeds practical URL limits.** A 16384-byte token and 8192-byte query sit above common defaults (nginx 8k header buffers). Emission only omits capture above the plan's limits, so URLs between roughly 8k and 16k would be emitted and then rejected with 414 by the front end. Research explicitly declined to invent a browser limit; a lower practical default or a documented note is still warranted.
- **LOW. `cv_cancel_key=None` semantics.** `cv_get_cls(None)` resolves to the current view (`base.py:501-502`), so "cancel to self" is what the plan preserves. Fine, but the docs in Plan 04 should state it, because D-09's missing-fallback error must not fire for this legal value.
- **LOW. Performance.** For nested no-object destinations the target adapter resolves the URL parent via `cv_get_parent_object` (`base.py:864-876`), one extra `get_object_or_404` per form render. Acceptable, but the form view already resolved that parent in Guardian dispatch; caching per request would avoid a duplicate query.

**Suggestions.**
- Strip the three navigation names at one request-level seam shared by list and card views (a small mixin, or in `CrudView.setup`), and let only `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get` do the session work. Add a card round trip to `test_cancel_state.py` that asserts the order form hidden inputs and pagination `base_qs` contain no `cv_from_*` names.
- Decide the golden policy now and write it into the plan and CHANGELOG: either regenerate the four action-menu goldens as the phase's declared markup change, or make `test_action_menus_golden.py` normalize `cv_from_*` query parameters before comparison. Do not leave this to Plan 05.
- Add `tests/test1/test_action_menus_golden.py tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py tests/test1/test_authorization_templates.py tests/test1/test_authorization_matrix.py` to task 01-02's verify, and list those test files in `files_modified` if their exact-link assertions are to be migrated to path-plus-parsed-query assertions.
- Split 01-01: tracer = emit key plus token, resolve, render through crispy, visit, assert same records. Move the full bound/limit matrix and the session clear-on-empty behavior into 01-03 where its tests already live.

**Risk Assessment: HIGH.** The design is right, but the plan under-reports its own blast radius (goldens, card pages, cross-suite exact links), and the omissions land on later plans that do not own the files.

## 06.7-02

**Summary.** The permission layer reuses the right hooks: core `cv_has_access` at `base.py:926-930` denies a list fallback to a user holding `change` but not `view`, and the Guardian object, queryset and parent policies at `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:78-92,129-131,302-337` are the ones the plan names. The MRO it depends on holds: every Guardian composite class puts `GuardianParentPermissionMixin` first (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:32-34,38-40,230-235,245-254`), so a classmethod on the parent mixin calling `super()` reaches the object or queryset adapter and then core. The plan's one factual claim about the codebase is wrong, and it is the claim the whole diagnostic hangs on.

**Strengths.**
- Access-before-state ordering and the "which object does the state hook receive" rule match the existing adapters: `base.py:368-379` and the Guardian create override at `views.py:298-307` already model a state object that differs from the page object.
- "Empty permitted queryset is still accessible" is exactly `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_has_access` (`mixins.py:129-131`), so the plan is not inventing policy.
- Reusing `_guardian_user_has_parent_perm` (`mixins.py:302-323`) keeps the None-key and accept-global semantics that `test_guardian_parent_none.py` already pins (for example lines 259-275, 792-806).
- The discriminating case "form allowed by parent create permission, list denied by parent view permission" is real: `_guardian_resolve_parent_perm_key` at `mixins.py:287-297` picks a different key for `cv_permission == "add"`.
- Catching only `Http404` from parent resolution mirrors `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_get_context` at `mixins.py:169-177`.

**Concerns.**
- **HIGH. `viewset.E255` is already taken.** `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:383-392` yields `CheckExpression(... id="E255" ...)` for misconfigured context-button targets, and check ids render as `viewset.<id>` (`src/crud_views/lib/check.py:37`). The plan's `<verification>` states "The E255 identifier was absent from inspected source at planning time", which is false. Reusing it would make the new diagnostic indistinguishable from the context-button error in `manage.py check` output, break any `--deselect`/silence by id, and the id also propagates into Plan 04's docs and the Phase 7 inventory. E250 through E255 are in use; E256 is free.
- **MEDIUM. Startup scope of the new check is unverified against existing fixtures.** The check reports "explicit static forms with missing fallback" and auto forms with no usable origin. Any registered test ViewSet with a form view but neither list nor card would start failing system checks the moment this lands. I could not enumerate the registry in this sandbox; the executor must do so before choosing Error over Warning severity.
- **MEDIUM. Dependency ordering with Plan 01.** This plan's verify includes `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py`, which Plan 01's default change already breaks at line 875 (see 06.7-01). The plan does not list that file in `files_modified`, so the executor either edits an unowned file or reports a failure it cannot fix.
- **LOW. `-k 'core or fallback or diagnostic'`** relies on test naming discipline in a file that does not exist yet; a mis-named test silently drops out of the gate.

**Suggestions.**
- Use `E256` (or another free id) and update Plan 04's "E255/runtime ImproperlyConfigured" wording and the Phase 7 handoff accordingly.
- Add a one-line enumeration step to 02-01: list every registered ViewSet whose views include `CrudViewProcessFormMixin` but register neither `list` nor `card`, and record the result in the SUMMARY before the check's severity is final.
- Move repair of `test_guardian_parent_none.py:875` (and any other card-page exact-link assertion) into Plan 01-02, or add the file to this plan's `files_modified` with an explicit note.

**Risk Assessment: MEDIUM.** The authorization design is faithful to existing policy. The risk is the id collision, which is cheap to fix now and expensive once documented and frozen.

## 06.7-03

**Summary.** Selector forwarding and the template tag are both well scoped. `PolymorphicCreateSelectView` at `src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:24` extends `CrudView, generic.FormView`, not the form mixin, so the explicit `cv_cancel_keys="auto"` the plan prescribes is required rather than redundant. `form_valid` at lines 68-72 reverses only the create route, confirming the research gap. The tag design correctly refuses the two things the existing `cv_context_url` does (`templatetags/crud_views.py:281-292`): generic action-link generation that would recapture the form as an origin, and the `ignore_exception(ViewSetKeyFoundError)` decorator that would swallow the D-09 configuration error.

**Strengths.**
- Forwarding the original token rather than recapturing is the right call: the selector and the concrete create share ViewSet identity and parent kwargs, so a token bound to the list origin stays valid for the create target under Plan 01's binding rules.
- "Drop capture and key together when invalid" avoids the half-trusted state Plan 01 forbids.
- Surface parity is tested from the authoritative view object, which `form.py:69-77` already prefers over `ModelForm.instance`.
- The modal 422 path is already covered by `test_modal.py:261-277`; extending it to repeated posts is cheap.
- The selector page itself renders no cancel control (`view_create.content.html` renders the form through `cv_render_form`, and `PolymorphicContentTypeForm` has no helper), so making it a cancel owner only affects link emission. The plan does not claim otherwise.

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM. Parallel execution hazard.** 03-02 says any failing core behavior "is a dependency defect to repair in its owning plan". Plan 02 edits `base.py` in the same wave. If 03 surfaces a resolver bug, the fix lands in a file another in-flight worktree is modifying. The VALIDATION.md "zero file overlap" claim is true for declared files but not for the repair path the plan itself prescribes.
- **LOW. `cv_cancel_url` defaults.** The tag takes `obj=None` and uses the view object. For `CustomFormNoObjectView` the view has no `object`; the plan says no-object owners ignore supplied objects, which is consistent, but the tag should not raise when `view.object` is absent. Worth one explicit test.
- **LOW. `PolymorphicCreateView` URL kwargs.** `src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create.py:13` subclasses `CreateView`, so it is auto. Reversing the list destination from it needs only parent kwargs, but the invalid-create re-render posts to `request.get_full_path`, so the forwarded token must be on the create URL, not only in the selector's redirect `Location`. The plan says "appending to the reversed concrete create URL", which is correct; a test following the 302 is the proof.

**Suggestions.**
- Replace "repair in its owning plan" with an explicit rule: if 03 finds a core defect while 02 is in flight, stop and report; the orchestrator sequences the fix after 02 merges.
- Add a no-object custom form render of the tag to the parity test.

**Risk Assessment: LOW.** Narrow, well-bounded, and the only real risk is procedural.

## 06.7-04

**Summary.** The documentation targets are real and currently contradict the new contract: `docs/reference/update_view.md:120-124` recommends `{% cv_context_url view.cv_get_cancel_key as url %}` and states "The list loses its state", and `docs/reference/settings.md:31` describes the origin parameter as key-only. The synced example `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py:93-98` declares `cv_cancel_keys = ["list", "detail"]` and that exact block is mirrored at `update_view.md:62-70` under a `cv-sync` marker enforced by `examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py:86-91`. The plan accounts for all of this. It inherits the E255 error from Plan 02.

**Strengths.**
- Executable recipes follow the established pattern in `tests/test1/test_form_button_docs.py` (fence extraction, locmem loader, hostile literals), so the new docs test has a working template to copy.
- Pairing CHANGELOG Breaking with `docs/migrating-1.0.md` is required by CLAUDE.md and the plan makes it a tested assertion rather than a checklist item.
- Documenting "signatures do not encrypt" and browser-visible query data closes T-06.7-04 from Plan 01 explicitly.
- Keeping `cv_get_cancel_key` documented as a retained helper matches CONTEXT's "does not independently authorize removal".

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM. Inherits the wrong check id.** 04-01 documents "E255/runtime ImproperlyConfigured" and 04-03 inventories "E255" for Phase 7. Both must change with Plan 02.
- **LOW. `docs/reference/buttons.md:618`** mentions `get_cancel_button_context()` returning a URL; with the context now carrying `cv_url=None` for omission, that sentence becomes inaccurate and the file is not in `files_modified`.
- **LOW. SECRET_KEY rotation.** Signed tokens become invalid after key rotation (safe fallback), and `Signer` honours `SECRET_KEY_FALLBACKS` on the 4.2 floor. The migration section should say so in one sentence; nothing in the plan mentions rotation.
- **LOW. Example change ripples into the tutorial.** Removing `cv_cancel_keys` from `AuthorUpdateView` at `views.py:97` changes the synced block; `docs/getting_started/` tutorials that paste that class are exempt from sync only when unmarked, but should be checked for the same comment text.

**Suggestions.**
- Add `docs/reference/buttons.md` to `files_modified` and assert its cancel-context sentence in `test_cancel_docs.py`.
- Add a rotation sentence to the migration section and a grep assertion for it.

**Risk Assessment: LOW.** Mechanically sound; its correctness depends on Plans 01 and 02 settling names first, which the wave order guarantees.

## 06.7-05

**Summary.** The harness pattern it copies is mature: `tests/browser/theme-adoption.cjs` already owns a loopback Django process, records runtime identity, counts POSTs during cancel activation (lines 312-324), and cleans up only owned processes (lines 421-426); `presentation.cjs:396-404` already asserts the source-bound `crud_views.__file__` and a supported Django series. The final gate list matches the research matrix and the three cached interpreters exist (`.nox/tests-3-12-django-5-2`, `.nox/tests-3-12-django-4-2`, `.nox/crispy_floor-3-12-django-6-0`). The plan's weakness is that it is where the earlier plans' deferred breakage would surface.

**Strengths.**
- Tab A / Tab B isolation in one browser context is the right real-browser proof of D-06; request-level tests cannot show two concurrent pages sharing a session cookie.
- "No POST during cancel" is already an established assertion style (`theme-adoption.cjs:316-321`), and `tests/js/viewset.test.js:145-162` covers the delegated handler in jsdom, so the browser case adds the missing real-navigation half rather than duplicating.
- The 6.0 floor run keeps exactly the one documented deselect; no new exclusions.
- Human checks are kept distinct from automation and the acceptance file records runtime identity, which Phase 7 and the verifier will need.

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM. Deferred repair of old-default assertions.** 05-02 instructs repairing "that exact assertion with explicit static configuration only when the fixture's purpose is static" and forbids regenerating goldens. With the four action-menu goldens and the card-page exact-link assertions identified under 06.7-01, this plan would be the first full-suite run to see them and has no production or golden files in `files_modified`. Either the failures are fixed in wave 1 or this plan cannot reach green without editing unowned files.
- **LOW. Contradictory handling of a missing browser runtime.** The precondition says an unavailable runtime is "reported as unverified rather than auto-installed", but the smoke `fails_when` says failure when "runtime/source identity is absent". `presentation.cjs:441-444` already distinguishes `infrastructure_prerequisite_failed` (exit 2) from a real failure (exit 1); the new harness should adopt that convention so the acceptance file can say "unverified" truthfully.
- **LOW. Unverified precondition.** I could not inspect the Playwright/Chromium cache from this sandbox; RESEARCH.md says Node 24 and Playwright are cached. Treat as an open item for the executor.
- **LOW. Companion theme.** Plan 06.6 delivered a Tabler companion whose `cancelNavigation` case reads `data-cv-cancel-url` on `/create/` with no origin (fallback URL, unchanged). D-13's immediate template migration may still affect any copied cancel template in that companion. No plan mentions re-running the companion acceptance; Phase 7's handoff should at least record it as not re-run.

**Suggestions.**
- Pull the golden and exact-link repairs into Plan 01 and keep 05-02's rule as a backstop only.
- Mirror the `infrastructure_prerequisite_failed` exit-code convention from `presentation.cjs`.
- Add one line to the acceptance template for "companion acceptance: not re-run / re-run with result".

**Risk Assessment: MEDIUM.** The verification design is strong; the risk is that it is positioned to absorb problems the earlier plans should have owned.

## Cross-plan assessment

**Dependency ordering.** Wave order (01 → 02 ∥ 03 → 04 → 05) is correct for the declared files. Two ordering defects cut across it: Plan 01's default change breaks tests that only Plan 02 (`test_guardian_parent_none.py`) and Plan 05 (full suite, goldens) run, and Plan 03's "repair in owning plan" rule collides with Plan 02 editing `base.py` in the same wave.

**Phase goals.** Taken together the plans do achieve the ROADMAP success criteria: all five flows, repeated and empty query state, tab independence via a per-form signed capture, invalid-POST and selector continuity, permission-aware omission with distinct configuration errors, one URL helper across crispy, anchor, modal and custom templates, and paired Breaking/migration docs. CAN-01 through CAN-07 and D-01 through D-16 each have an owning task.

**Scope.** No scope creep. The signed transport with seven bounds is heavier than a plain structured parameter, but `security_enforcement` is on and the bounds give the DoS and tampering tests something concrete to assert. The one addition to the public surface that Phase 7 must freeze and that no decision names explicitly is the derived `cv_from_restore` parameter; Plan 04 documents it, which is sufficient.

**Security.** The threat registers are consistent and the mitigations trace to real seams. Nothing in the plans accepts a return URL, and reversal stays on `cv_get_url` with current parent kwargs (`base.py:549-555`). Signing adds integrity only; the plans say so.

**Overall risk: HIGH until two items are fixed, then MEDIUM.**
1. Plan 02 must not reuse `viewset.E255` (`buttons.py:392`); Plans 04 and the Phase 7 handoff must follow the new id.
2. Plan 01 must own the default change's blast radius: a consumer for the restore marker on card pages (`card.py:17` has no filter mixin), an explicit golden policy for the four action-menu goldens, and repair or migration of the exact-link assertions in the seven files listed above, with those files added to its verify commands.

Secondary items, in order of value: shrink the 01-01 tracer, add the enumeration of form-only ViewSets before fixing the new check's severity, resolve the parallel-repair rule between 02 and 03, and adopt the prerequisite-failure exit code in the browser harness.

## OpenCode Review (grok-review)

I'll verify each plan against the source, starting with the cancel, session, and rendering seams the plans depend on.
E255 is already a context-button check. I'll read the cancel, session, and Guardian seams next to see whether the plans collide with that.
## 06.7-01

The vertical slice is the right shape: one signed per-form capture, sibling reversal only, and an explicit empty-query marker before `ListViewTableFilterMixin` restores session memory. It will not work if `"auto"` is stored on `cv_cancel_keys` and the existing key helpers are left as they are.

**Strengths**
- Emission already goes through `cv_get_link_url` for row, context, and card actions (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:712`, `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:103`, `src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:77`). A tracer that follows a rendered Update link will exercise the real path.
- Invalid POST already keeps the query: create, update, custom, and delete templates post to `request.get_full_path`.
- Session writes already go through `SessionData.__exit__` (`src/crud_views/lib/session.py:71-80`), so clearing `filter_query_string` inside the existing `with` block will persist.
- Detail identity stays on the form object. `cv_get_url` puts `obj.pk` in the path (`base.py:541-542`); keeping the PK out of the token matches that.

**Concerns**
- **HIGH** — `"auto"` is not a key list, and the current helpers iterate it as one. `cv_get_link_origin_key` unpacks `cls.cv_cancel_keys or ()` (`base.py:796`). A non-empty string unpacks to characters, so `self.cv_key in keys` is false for `"list"` and no origin is emitted. `cv_get_allowed_origin_cls` uses `origin not in keys` (`base.py:745`), which is a substring test: a one-letter key such as `"a"` is in `"auto"`. `cv_origin_keys_registered` likewise iterates the string (`base.py:518-524`). The plan says E252 must accept `"auto"`, but it never names these three call sites. The tracer fails until all three resolve `effective_cancel_keys()` first.
- **MEDIUM** — Disabled persistence returns before any cleaning (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:364-365`). The task says to strip navigation fields in that branch too, but the failure statement only requires the session blob to stay unchanged, so a leftover `restore=1` in `request.GET` can still pass.
- **MEDIUM** — Card lists do not use `ListViewTableFilterMixin` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17`). URL capture can still restore a card query, but no task follows a filtered card link. CAN-02's card half is untested.
- **LOW** — `request.GET` is cached from `QUERY_STRING`. Cleaning one and not invalidating the other leaves the restore marker in django-filter and pagination links.

**Suggestions**
- In the same change that sets `CrudViewProcessFormMixin.cv_cancel_keys = "auto"`, route `cv_get_link_origin_key`, `cv_get_cancel_key`, and E252 through `effective_cancel_keys()`. Never pass the raw string to `in` or `for`.
- Add one filtered card → update → Cancel request next to the list tracer.
- Assert the disabled-persistence response query no longer contains the restore or cancel parameter.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** The transport and session rules match the code. The `"auto"` string is a real foot-gun in the helpers the plan relies on, and the tracer should catch a missed emission fix only if those helpers are actually updated.

## 06.7-02

The three-way outcome (usable URL, omit, `ImproperlyConfigured`) matches the current gap: `get_cancel_button_context` accepts `user` and never uses it (`base.py:817-824`). The new check id does not.

**Strengths**
- Calling the target class, not the form class, is the right seam. `GuardianListViewPermissionRequired` is `(GuardianParentPermissionMixin, GuardianQuerysetMixin, ListViewPermissionRequired)` and create is parent-only (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:245-257`), so parent-create permission cannot be inferred from list access.
- `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_has_access` returns true for any non-inactive user (`mixins.py:129-131`), including an empty permitted queryset. The plan correctly refuses to treat that as denial.
- Anonymous list entry is not `cv_has_access`: that method returns true for anonymous users, while `has_permission` redirects, 403s, or 404s (`mixins.py:118-127`). Requiring the extension policy here is necessary.
- Access-before-state matches `cv_has_access` then `cv_action_enabled` (`base.py:357-366`). Nested no-object state uses the URL parent, same as `cv_get_action_object` (`base.py:381-388`) and `_guardian_check_parent` (`mixins.py:330-336`).

**Concerns**
- **HIGH** — `viewset.E255` is already taken. `Check.get_id` prefixes `viewset.` (`src/crud_views/lib/check.py:33-37`). `context_button_checks` yields `id="E255"` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:377-392`). `tests/test1/test_context_button_check.py:1` documents that id. E256–E259 are unused; E254 is delete success (`src/crud_views/lib/views/delete.py:112-114`). The plan's claim that E255 was absent is false. Reusing it makes the cancel diagnostic indistinguishable from broken context buttons, and plan 04 then documents the wrong id.
- **MEDIUM** — `cv_get_parent_object` asserts when there is no parent (`base.py:861-868`) and raises `Http404` only for a missing row. Catching `Http404` is not enough; the adapter must not call it unless `has_parent` is true, or a top-level target turns a programming error into a hidden Cancel.
- **LOW** — Create's state adapter is `_cv_action_enabled_for_rendering`, not raw `cv_action_enabled` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/create.py:51-70`). Standard list/card/detail targets do not use that override. An explicit custom key of `"create"` will disagree with request enforcement unless the adapter calls the rendering hook.

**Suggestions**
- Allocate a free id (E256) and grep `viewset.E255` before adding the check.
- Guard `cv_get_parent_object` with `has_parent`. Let `AssertionError` and unexpected query errors propagate.
- For custom keys, call `_cv_action_enabled_for_rendering(user, obj, rendering_view)` instead of inventing a second state-object rule.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** The Guardian matrix matches the mixins. Shipping it under E255 collides with a public check id.

## 06.7-03

Selector forwarding and the new tag are the right seams, and they do not overlap plan 02's files. The selector is not a form-mixin view, so `"auto"` has to be set on that class itself.

**Strengths**
- `PolymorphicCreateSelectView.form_valid` reverses create with only `polymorphic_ctype_id` and drops the query (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:68-72`). Appending the already validated token, and not calling `cv_get_link_url` on the selector, is the correct fix.
- `PolymorphicCreateView` subclasses `CreateView` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create.py:13`), so the concrete form inherits plan 01's mixin once the token arrives.
- The create template posts to `get_full_path`, so an invalid type selection keeps the original query without a new template.
- Modal 422 reads `form.action` (`src/crud_views/static/crud_views/js/modal.js:165-187`). That action is `get_full_path`, so a re-rendered body still sees the capture.
- `cv_context_url` is the wrong helper: it resolves a generic action and is wrapped in `@ignore_exception` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:281-292`). A dedicated tag that does not swallow `ImproperlyConfigured` is required.
- Native anchors do not carry `data-cv-cancel-url`; only `<button>` does (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/tags/button/form.html:1-3`). `viewset.js:37-44` will not steal anchor navigation. Omission has to happen before `Button(...)`, because clearing the URL falls back to `<button>` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:353-357`).

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM** — `CrispyDeleteForm.helper` inlines `get_cancel_button()` and does not use `get_form_actions` (`src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py:138-149`). The plan names that path. Subclasses that copy it and pass `None` into `FormActions` are a breaking return-type change that this task does not test outside the package.
- **LOW** — The selector does not inherit `CrudViewProcessFormMixin` (`create_select.py:24`). Setting `"auto"` there depends on plan 01 treating the string as a general value, not a mixin-only flag. That coupling is real and unstated.
- **LOW** — Protected delete already omits the form (`src/crud_views/templates/crud_views/view_delete.content.html:15-22`). The preservation test is right; it does not need a new control.

**Suggestions**
- Forward with `urlencode` onto the reversed path. Do not put the token in `extra_kwargs` or `reverse` will reject it.
- Add one subclass that overrides `get_form_actions` / the delete helper and still filters `None`.
- Compare decoded query values, not the raw `data-cv-cancel-url` regex in `tests/test1/test_cancel_origin.py:21-25`.

**Risk: LOW.** The selector and tag changes match the current call graph, assuming plan 01's `"auto"` contract is finished first.

## 06.7-04

The reference pages named in the plan are the ones that still teach the key-only recipe. The edit set misses the other copies of that contract, and the phase gate will not notice.

**Strengths**
- `docs/reference/update_view.md:120-124` still recommends `{% cv_context_url view.cv_get_cancel_key as url %}`, and line 121 says returning to the list drops filter, sort, and page. Replacing that is the actual migration.
- Pairing a `### Breaking` entry with `docs/migrating-1.0.md` matches the repo rule.
- Keeping `cv_get_cancel_key` as a key helper, not the URL contract, matches the decision not to delete the method.

**Concerns**
- **HIGH** — `docs/faq.md:221-229` is a `cv-sync` copy of `AuthorUpdateView`, and line 247 still says the list loses filter, sort, and page. Plan 04-03 removes `cv_cancel_keys` from `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py:97`. `examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py` requires that fence to appear verbatim. `faq.md` is not in `files_modified`. `card-list-view.md` still describes key-only cancel. Plan 05 runs `pytest tests`, not the example sync suite, so this ships stale.
- **MEDIUM** — `examples/bootstrap5/library/tests.py:65-79` asserts `href="{update_url}?cv_from=detail"` and a cancel URL with no capture token. Plan 01 appends a token for every dynamic cancel, including this explicit list. No plan updates or runs that file.
- **MEDIUM** — `examples/bootstrap5/project/features.py:81-82` says `cv_cancel_keys` on `AuthorUpdateView` is what sends Cancel back. Removing the attribute makes that sentence false. It is not in the plan.
- **LOW** — Attribute tables in the four reference pages still type `cv_cancel_keys` as `list[str] | None` with default `None` (`update_view.md:51`, `create_view.md:59`). `"auto"` has to be in those tables or the docs contradict the mixin.

**Suggestions**
- Add `docs/faq.md`, `docs/reference/card-list-view.md`, and `examples/bootstrap5/project/features.py` to this plan. Rewrite the FAQ limit that says list state is dropped.
- Update `library/tests.py` to parse the capture, and run `examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py` plus `library/tests.py` in task 04-03.
- If the example keeps an explicit list for the sync fence, say so. Do not remove the line from the class and leave the fences behind.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** The new recipe is right. The published surface is larger than the file list, and the listed gates do not cover it.

## 06.7-05

A source-bound browser run is justified: button Cancel is not a submit. It is a delegated click. The harness must not be copied from the Tabler companion runner.

**Strengths**
- `viewset.js:37-44` does `preventDefault` and `location.href` for `[data-cv-cancel-url]`. A click test that only loads HTML will not navigate. Asserting no POST and no row change is the right proof that this handler did not submit.
- Anchors navigate without that handler (`form.html:1-2`), so the native-link case is a separate path. The plan covers both.
- Isolating the database and recording the `crud_views` import path matches the earlier stale-install failure mode.
- Not treating `.venv` Django 6.1 as the 6.0 floor is correct.

**Concerns**
- **MEDIUM** — `tests/browser/theme-adoption.cjs` is companion-locked (recorded root, Django 6.1, fixed ports). Copying it will not boot this package. `presentation_server.py` is the closer pattern. The plan lists both and does not say which to follow.
- **MEDIUM** — The final commands never run `examples/bootstrap5`. That is where `test_docs_sync.py` and `library/tests.py:65-79` live. Plan 04's doc drift is invisible here.
- **MEDIUM** — Task 05-02 allows "repair that exact assertion with explicit static configuration" when a fixture fails. Plan 01 says not to opt shared fixtures out of the new default. Applied loosely, this turns default-behavior failures into static overrides and the new tests never see them.
- **LOW** — Button clicks need `viewset.js` and `{% cv_config %}`. A minimal fixture server that omits them makes the button case a false failure, or a skipped click that never hits the resolver.

**Suggestions**
- Start from `tests/browser/presentation_server.py`, not `theme-adoption.cjs`. Load package static files and assert `location.href` changes without a POST.
- Add the example sync and library cancel tests to the final gate.
- Limit static-config repairs to fixtures whose docstring purpose is static cancel. Fail the run if a default form view is opted out.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** The browser proof is aimed at the real click path. The harness source and the example-suite hole are the parts that can report success without covering the contract.

## Cross-plan

The five plans do cover CAN-01–CAN-07 if the `"auto"` helpers, the check id, and the doc fences are fixed before execution. Wave 2 can stay parallel: plan 02 touches `base.py` and Guardian mixins; plan 03 touches the selector and the template tag.

The shared contract is otherwise sound. Success keeps the existing `cv_from` channel (`src/crud_views/lib/settings.py:39`) and does not gain the captured query. Protected delete still has no form. Signing is integrity only, and destination authorization stays on the target request.

Three gaps cross plans. `"auto"` must be resolved before any membership test, or plan 03's selector assignment and plan 01's emission both mis-handle it. E255 cannot be the cancel check, or plan 04 documents a lie. The example and FAQ still assert key-only URLs and "the list loses its state"; plans 04 and 05 never execute those tests.

**Overall risk: MEDIUM.** No open product decision is blocking. Do not start implementation on E255 or on a raw `"auto"` string in the existing key helpers.

## OpenCode Review (gemini-review)

## 06.7-01

### Summary
Plan `06.7-01` introduces the core dynamic-cancel transport (`src/crud_views/lib/view/cancel.py`), the URL-level resolver `CrudView.cv_get_cancel_url()` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py`), automatic standard-form defaults via `CrudViewProcessFormMixin.cv_cancel_keys = "auto"` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py`), crispy action-group omission (`src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py`), and explicit list-state restoration before session fallback. While the signed envelope design and separation from `cv_success_keys` are well-structured, source verification reveals several high-severity integration gaps: (1) cleaning `cv_from_restore=1` only in `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get` leaves non-filter `ListView` and `CardListView` classes (including `AuthorListView`, which Task `01-01` targets) leaking `cv_from_restore=1` into `request.GET`, `META["QUERY_STRING"]`, pagination links, table sort links, and `CardOrderMixin` hidden inputs; (2) implementing permission-aware omission (`_cv_cancel_target_available`) and `cv_cancel_keys = "auto"` in Wave 1 immediately breaks single-permission fixtures in `tests/test1/test_cancel_origin.py` as well as downstream golden/menu suites that are not in `files_modified`; and (3) fallback resolution on object-based views (`cv_cancel_key = "detail"`) does not guard against unsaved UUID model instances whose `pk` is already populated (`_state.adding is True`).

### Strengths
- **Clean MRO placement for automatic form defaults**: Setting `cv_cancel_keys = "auto"` on `CrudViewProcessFormMixin` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:23`) while keeping `CrudView.cv_cancel_keys = None` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:82`) cleanly targets `CreateView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/create.py:12`), `UpdateView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/update.py:9`), `DeleteView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/delete.py:51`), `CustomFormView` and `CustomFormNoObjectView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/form.py:10,40`), and `WorkflowView` (`src/crud_views_workflow/lib/views.py:18`), because `CrudViewProcessFormMixin` precedes `CrudView` in every standard form view's MRO while leaving non-form views (`ListView`, `DetailView`, `CardListView`, `ActionView`, `ManageView`) at `None`.
- **JSON-serializable `"auto"` sentinel compatible with metadata and ManageView**: Using the literal string `"auto"` instead of an opaque `object()` sentinel ensures `CrudView.cv_get_dict()` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:477`) and `ManageView.get_view_data()` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/manage.py:111`) remain serializable and readable without custom encoders.
- **Authoritative object selection in crispy forms**: Filtering `None` from `CrispyFormMixin.get_form_actions()` (`src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py:57-58`) and `CrispyDeleteForm.helper` (`src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py:139-149`) rather than passing `data_cv_cancel_url=None` into `Button` avoids the low-level leaf fallback in `_form_button_data()` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:354-357`), which intentionally renders a URL-less `<button type="button">` when `cancel_url` is `None`.
- **Bounded, uncompressed signed payload**: Rejecting compressed tokens before `Signer.unsign_object()` and bounding token length, JSON size, and `parse_qsl(..., max_num_fields=128, keep_blank_values=True)` prevents decompression bombs and parameter-pollution DoS while preserving multivalue and blank query parameters.

### Concerns
- **[HIGH] Required property — Internal navigation parameters (`cv_from_restore=1`) must not leak into rendered list/card pages or accumulate across non-filter list and card views (CAN-02, CAN-03, D-06)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:144-280,299-399`, `src/crud_views/lib/views/list.py:19-56`, `src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17-45`, `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:109,301-309,558-569`, and `tests/test1/app/views.py:63-80`.
  - **Mechanism**: `<interfaces>` specifies: *"Append the derived restore scalar with value 1 for accepted list/card captures, including empty query"*, and `files_modified` only includes `src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py` (where `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get` lives at lines 357–399), not `src/crud_views/lib/views/list.py` or `src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py`. However, `ListView` (`list.py:19`) and `CardListView` (`card.py:17`) do **not** inherit `ListViewTableFilterMixin` unless a view explicitly mixes it in. In fact, `AuthorListView` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:63`), `AuthorCardListView` (`views.py:74`), and `BookListView` (`views.py:410`) do not inherit `ListViewTableFilterMixin`. When a user cancels back to `AuthorListView` or `AuthorCardListView` at `/author/?cv_from_restore=1` (or with sorting/pagination), `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get` never runs:
    1. `request.GET` and `request.META["QUERY_STRING"]` retain `cv_from_restore=1`.
    2. `CardOrderMixin.get_context_data()` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:273-279`) copies every `request.GET` parameter except `{self.cv_order_param, self.cv_order_dir_param, "page"}` into `context["cv_order_preserved_params"]`, emitting `<input type="hidden" name="cv_from_restore" value="1">` into the card sort form.
    3. `cv_pagination` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:560-563`) copies `request.GET` (popping only `"page"`), and `cv_querystring` (`crud_views.py:301-309`) reads `request.GET`, so every pagination link and table sort header link on the restored page will carry `cv_from_restore=1`.
    4. Furthermore, in `WSGIRequest`, `request.GET` is a cached property (`request.__dict__["GET"]`) holding an immutable `QueryDict`. Even inside `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get`, if `request.GET` was already accessed during `dispatch`/`has_permission` (e.g., by a custom `cv_action_enabled` hook), mutating `request.META["QUERY_STRING"]` alone does not update `request.GET` unless `request.GET` is explicitly rebound to a cleaned immutable `QueryDict`.
- **[HIGH] Required property — Single-permission test fixtures in `test_cancel_origin.py` must still render Cancel when testing static fallback behavior (CAN-01, CAN-05, D-03, D-10)**:
  - **Evidence**: `tests/test1/conftest.py:210-217,250-252,335-346` and `tests/test1/test_cancel_origin.py:47-61`.
  - **Mechanism**: Task `01-01` wires `_cv_cancel_target_available` into `cv_get_cancel_url` and `get_cancel_button`, omitting the Cancel button when the user lacks `cv_has_access` on the fallback target (`"list"`). Task `01-02` instructs: *"Replace tests claiming unspecified form defaults are static with explicit None tests plus auto tests"* in `tests/test1/test_cancel_origin.py`. However, `test_default_without_cancel_keys_is_unchanged` (`test_cancel_origin.py:47`) uses `client_user_author_change`, and `test_default_without_cancel_keys_is_unchanged_int_pk` (`test_cancel_origin.py:57`) uses `client_user_publisher_change`. In `tests/test1/conftest.py:210-217` and `335-340`, `user_author_change` and `user_publisher_change` are granted **only** `"change"` permission and **not** `"view"` permission! Because `AuthorListView` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:63`) and `PublisherListView` (`views.py:299`) inherit `ListViewPermissionRequired` (`cv_permission = "view"`), `AuthorListView.cv_has_access(user_author_change, None)` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:926-930`) returns `False`. Thus, `cv_get_cancel_url()` returns `None`, the Cancel button is omitted, and `cancel_url(response)` (`test_cancel_origin.py:21-25`) fails with `AssertionError: no cancel button rendered` even when `cv_cancel_keys = None` is set!
- **[HIGH] Required property — Fallback resolution to an object-based view (`cls.cv_object is True`) must reject unsaved UUID model instances and missing objects instead of reversing a 404 URL (CAN-01, CAN-05, D-09, D-11)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:505-511,540-543,758-772` and `tests/test1/test_cancel_origin.py:143-153`.
  - **Mechanism**: In `CrudView.cv_get_cancel_key()` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:768-771`), origin validation checks `state = getattr(obj, "_state", None); unsaved = state is not None and state.adding; if cls.cv_object and (obj is None or unsaved): return self.cv_cancel_key`. However, when falling back to `self.cv_cancel_key` and calling `self.cv_get_url(key=fallback_key, obj=obj)` (`base.py:562-568`), `cv_get_cls_assert_object()` (`base.py:510`) only checks `bool(obj)` (`cls.cv_object is True and obj`), not `unsaved` or `obj.pk is not None`! For UUID-PK models like `Author`, an unsaved `Author()` instance (such as `form.instance` on `CreateView` via `CrispyFormMixin.get_cancel_button_kwargs` at `src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py:74-76`, or `unsaved` in `test_cancel_origin.py:152`) is truthy and already has a non-`None` UUID `pk`, with `obj._state.adding == True`. If `cv_cancel_key = "detail"` is configured on a no-object view or called with an unsaved instance, `cv_get_cancel_url` will neither omit nor raise `ImproperlyConfigured` unless the saved-object check (`obj is not None and not unsaved and getattr(obj, "pk", None) is not None`) is applied to the fallback target class as well as the origin class, and `obj` is forced to `None` when `not self.cv_object`.
- **[MEDIUM] Required property — Core `_cv_cancel_target_available` must not crash with `Http404` or `KeyError` when resolving a nested no-object target's URL parent (CAN-05, D-10, D-11)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:864-876`, `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:178-181`, and `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:169-177`.
  - **Mechanism**: `<interfaces>` states that in `CrudView._cv_cancel_target_available(cls, user, obj, rendering_view)`, `action_obj` is *"saved object, current URL parent for nested no-object destinations, otherwise None"*. Resolving the current URL parent via `rendering_view.cv_get_parent_object()` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:864-876`) indexes `self.kwargs[arg]` and calls `get_object_or_404(parent_model, pk=pk)`, which raises `KeyError` or `Http404`. Unlike Plan `06.7-02` Task `02-02` (which mentions catching `Http404` in Guardian), Plan `06.7-01` does not specify catching `(Http404, KeyError)` in core `CrudView._cv_cancel_target_available` when `cls.cv_viewset.has_parent` and `not cls.cv_object`, nor does it guard against calling `cv_get_parent_object()` twice when Guardian's `super()` chain runs.
- **[MEDIUM] Required property — Enabling `CrudViewProcessFormMixin.cv_cancel_keys = "auto"` in Wave 1 alters emitted `href` links in existing action-menu golden and list-menu tests not listed in `files_modified` (CAN-01, CAN-07)**:
  - **Evidence**: `tests/test1/test_action_menus_golden.py:105-143`, `tests/test1/golden/action_menus/list_full.html:1`, `tests/test1/golden/action_menus/toolbar_detail_full.html:1`, `tests/test1/golden/action_menus/toolbar_publisher_list.html:1`, `tests/test1/golden/action_menus/cards_author_full.html:1`, and `tests/test1/test_action_menus_list.py:61`.
  - **Mechanism**: Once `CrudViewProcessFormMixin.cv_cancel_keys = "auto"` is active, `AuthorCreateView`, `AuthorUpdateView`, `AuthorDeleteView`, and `PublisherCreateView` (`tests/test1/app/views.py:110-123,328-341`) become dynamic cancel owners. Consequently, `cv_get_link_url()` appends `?cv_from=...&cv_from_cancel=...` to the `create`, `update`, and `delete` links rendered on `AuthorListView`, `AuthorDetailView`, `AuthorCardListView`, and `PublisherListView`. This causes `test_action_menus_list.py:61` (`assert update.get("href").endswith("/update/")`) and 4 golden comparisons in `test_action_menus_golden.py` to fail unless those tests/fixtures are updated.

### Suggestions
- Strip internal navigation parameters (`cv_from`, `cv_from_cancel`, `cv_from_restore`) from `request.GET` (by rebinding `request.GET` to a cleaned immutable `QueryDict`) and `request.META["QUERY_STRING"]` in a shared `ListView` / `CardListView` request hook (or `CrudView.setup` / `dispatch` for list/card views and `CardOrderMixin.get_context_data`), not solely inside `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get`.
- In `tests/test1/test_cancel_origin.py:47-61`, grant `"view"` permission to the test user (or use a user with both `"view"` and `"change"` permissions) when testing static fallback resolution, and add a dedicated test verifying that a `"change"`-only user has the Cancel button omitted.
- Apply the saved-object usability check (`not self.cv_object` forces `obj = None`; if `target_cls.cv_object` is `True`, require `obj is not None`, `not getattr(getattr(obj, "_state", None), "adding", False)`, and `getattr(obj, "pk", None) is not None`) to both origin and fallback resolution in `cv_get_cancel_url()` and `cv_get_cancel_key()`.
- In `CrudView._cv_cancel_target_available`, catch `(Http404, KeyError)` when resolving `rendering_view.cv_get_parent_object()` and return `False` so a missing parent never crashes template rendering.

### Risk Assessment
**HIGH** — The plan modifies core link emission, cancel URL resolution, and form button rendering across all standard views. Without cleaning `cv_from_restore=1` on non-filter `ListView`/`CardListView` classes and accounting for single-permission test fixtures that lack `"view"` permission on the `"list"` fallback, both new and existing tests will fail.
