## 06.7-02

### Summary
Plan `06.7-02` adds startup/runtime diagnostics for missing or unusable configured cancel fallbacks (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py`) and implements Guardian's target-availability adapter `_cv_cancel_target_available` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py`) with permission/state matrix tests in `tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py`. Source inspection reveals three critical issues: (1) the plan selects system check ID `viewset.E255` and explicitly claims in `<verification>` that *"The E255 identifier was absent from inspected source at planning time"*, which is false — `viewset.E255` is already defined in `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:54,379,392` and tested in `tests/test1/test_context_button_check.py:1,9,19`; (2) `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_has_access` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:130-131`) returns `not _guardian_inactive_actor(user)`, which evaluates to `True` for `AnonymousUser`even though `has_permission()` denies anonymous users; and (3) composing `_cv_cancel_target_available` via `super()` across `GuardianParentPermissionMixin`, `GuardianQuerysetMixin` / `GuardianObjectPermissionMixin`, and `CrudView` will either double-evaluate `cv_action_enabled(user, parent_obj)` or wrongly invoke `CrudViewPermissionRequiredMixin.cv_has_access` (model-level permission) on nested child views where `GuardianParentPermissionMixin.has_permission` intentionally bypasses model-level permissions.

### Strengths
- **Clear separation of outcomes (D-09, D-10, D-11)**: Distinguishing malformed request origin data (fallback), registered-but-inaccessible destinations (omit Cancel, return `None`), and unregistered/unusable developer-configured `cv_cancel_key` (`ImproperlyConfigured` at runtime plus a startup check) prevents configuration bugs from being masked as permission denials.
- **No core-to-Guardian import coupling**: Dispatching `_cv_cancel_target_available(cls, user, obj, rendering_view)` as a classmethod on the target view class keeps `crud_views_guardian` logic inside `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py` without importing Guardian in `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py`.
- **Preservation of Guardian queryset entry semantics**: Recognizing that `GuardianListViewPermissionRequired` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:106-113,186-199`) gates access at the view level and filters rows in `get_queryset()` ensures that a permitted list with zero visible rows remains an accessible Cancel destination.

### Concerns
- **[HIGH] Required property — New system check IDs must not collide with existing registered check IDs (CAN-05, D-09, STAB-01)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:52-55,377-392` and `tests/test1/test_context_button_check.py:1-25,144-180`.
  - **Mechanism**: Task `02-01` instructs: *"Define new startup check `viewset.E255` in `CrudView.checks`, scoped to cancel-owning form classes"*, and `<verification>` asserts: *"The E255 identifier was absent from inspected source at planning time; preserve existing E252/E253/E254 semantics."* In reality, `viewset.E255` is already used in `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392` (`yield CheckExpression(context=context, id="E255", expression=problem is None, msg=problem or "")`) for unresolved `ContextButton` targets (#172) and is directly asserted by `tests/test1/test_context_button_check.py:9` (`E255 = "viewset.E255"`). Reusing `viewset.E255` creates an immediate check-ID collision (and breaks `test_context_button_check.py:157-189` whenever a `Probe` class without a `list` view runs `Probe.checks()` and filters by `m.id == "viewset.E255"`). The next unused check ID in the `E25x`/`E26x` series is `E256` (or `E265`).
- **[HIGH] Required property — Guardian target availability must match destination `dispatch()`/`has_permission()` for anonymous actors, inactive superusers, and nested child views (CAN-05, D-10, D-15)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:14-21,45-54,118-131,262-277,325-337`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:257-388`, and `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:925-930`.
  - **Mechanism**:
    1. **AnonymousUser false positive**: `_guardian_inactive_actor(user)` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:14-21`) returns `bool(getattr(user, "is_authenticated", False)) and not getattr(user, "is_active", True)`, which is `False` for `AnonymousUser`. Consequently, `GuardianQuerysetMixin.cv_has_access(AnonymousUser)` (`mixins.py:130-131`) returns `not False` = **`True`**! Yet `GuardianQuerysetMixin.has_permission()` (`mixins.py:119`), `GuardianObjectPermissionMixin.has_permission()` (`mixins.py:46`), and `GuardianParentPermissionMixin.dispatch()` (`mixins.py:270`) all deny unauthenticated users (`if not request.user.is_authenticated:`). Every Guardian `_cv_cancel_target_available` implementation must require `bool(getattr(user, "is_authenticated", False)) and not _guardian_inactive_actor(user)`.
    2. **Double `cv_action_enabled` invocation or wrong MRO fallthrough**: For `GuardianListViewPermissionRequired(GuardianParentPermissionMixin, GuardianQuerysetMixin, ListViewPermissionRequired)` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:245-247`), the MRO is `GuardianParentPermissionMixin -> GuardianQuerysetMixin -> ListViewPermissionRequired -> CrudViewPermissionRequiredMixin -> ListView -> CrudView`. If `GuardianParentPermissionMixin._cv_cancel_target_available` checks parent permission and calls `super()._cv_cancel_target_available(user, obj, rendering_view)`, and `GuardianQuerysetMixin` also calls `super()`, execution reaches `CrudView._cv_cancel_target_available`, which calls `cls.cv_has_access(user, target_obj)` and `cls.cv_action_enabled(user, action_obj)`. If both `GuardianParentPermissionMixin` and `CrudView` resolve `parent_obj` and call `cls.cv_action_enabled(user, parent_obj)`, `cv_action_enabled` is called **twice** per render (breaking `hook_calls` assertions like `tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py:274` and violating Task `02-02`'s requirement *"Call action state once"*).
    3. **Custom no-object child views under `GuardianParentPermissionMixin`**: In `GuardianParentPermissionMixin.has_permission()` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:264-265`), when `self.cv_viewset.parent is not None`, `has_permission()` returns `True` so `CrudViewPermissionRequiredMixin`'s model-level check (`base.py:913-930`) is bypassed. Moreover, `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired.cv_has_access(user, None)` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:381-382`) unconditionally returns `False` when `obj is None`! If `_cv_cancel_target_available` delegates to `cls.cv_has_access(user, None)` for a nested no-object target, it will falsely deny access unless `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired` (or `GuardianParentPermissionMixin` for parent-only child views) checks `cls.cv_create_has_access(user, rendering_view, parent_obj)` / `_guardian_user_has_parent_perm(user, parent_obj)`.
- **[MEDIUM] Required property — Startup fallback check must still flag an explicitly misconfigured `cv_cancel_key` even when dynamic origins exist (CAN-05, D-09)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:81,173-184` and `src/crud_views/lib/views/delete.py:113-136`.
  - **Mechanism**: Task `02-01` states: *"report missing effective configured fallback when no registered usable allowed origin exists; explicit static forms with missing fallback always report."* If a developer explicitly sets `cv_cancel_key = "typo"` on `UpdateView` (or `cv_cancel_key = "detail"` on `CreateView`, where `"detail"` is an object view unusable by `CreateView`) in a ViewSet that has `"list"` registered, `"list"` is a registered usable allowed origin under `cv_cancel_keys = "auto"`. Skipping the startup check whenever *any* allowed origin exists means explicit `cv_cancel_key` typos or object-view fallbacks on no-object forms pass `manage.py check` silently and only blow up with `ImproperlyConfigured` at runtime when a user visits the form URL directly without `?cv_from=list`. Only the unoverridden default `cv_cancel_key = "list"` on a detail-only ViewSet (where `"detail"` is a valid dynamic origin for `UpdateView`/`DeleteView`) should be exempted at startup.

### Suggestions
- Change the new system check ID from `viewset.E255` to `viewset.E256` (or `viewset.E265`) across Plans `06.7-02` and `06.7-04`, since `viewset.E255` is already in use in `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392`.
- In `CrudView.checks`, always report `E256` when `cls.cv_cancel_key` is explicitly overridden to an unregistered or unusable view (e.g., `target_cls.cv_object and not cls.cv_object`), and only excuse the unoverridden default `cv_cancel_key == "list"` when `cls.cv_object is True` and at least one usable allowed origin (e.g. `"detail"`) is registered.
- In `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py`, make `_cv_cancel_target_available` check `if not getattr(user, "is_authenticated", False) or _guardian_inactive_actor(user): return False` first, validate `_guardian_child_composition_error()`, resolve `parent_obj` via `rendering_view.cv_get_parent_object()` catching `(Http404, KeyError)` once when `cls.cv_viewset.parent is not None`, check `cls._guardian_user_has_parent_perm(user, parent_obj)`, and evaluate `cls.cv_action_enabled` **exactly once** with `obj` (for `cls.cv_object`) or `parent_obj` (for nested no-object views) or `None` (for top-level no-object views).

### Risk Assessment
**HIGH** — Reusing `viewset.E255` is a guaranteed collision with existing system check `viewset.E255` in `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392`, and the Guardian MRO/anonymous-user subtleties will cause authorization mismatches if not explicitly handled.

## 06.7-03

### Summary
Plan `06.7-03` forwards captured cancel state through `PolymorphicCreateSelectView` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py`) and exposes the `{% cv_cancel_url %}` template tag (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py`), verifying parity across crispy buttons, native `<a>` cancel links, custom templates, and modal 422 responses. Source verification exposes three significant issues: (1) Task `03-01`'s verification command runs `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py`, which currently fails at `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py:174` once permission-aware Cancel omission is enabled because `client_user_author_delete` lacks `"view"` permission; (2) Task `03-01` has an internal protocol contradiction when `cv_from_cancel` is tampered/invalid on `PolymorphicCreateSelectView` while `PolymorphicCreateView` has `cv_success_keys = ["list"]` AND `cv_cancel_keys = "auto"`; and (3) `PolymorphicCreateSelectView` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:24`) inherits `(CrudView, generic.FormView)` rather than `CrudViewProcessFormMixin`, so its behavior and link emission from `VehicleListView` (`cv_context_actions = ["create_select"]`) require careful coordination with `PolymorphicCreateView`.

### Strengths
- **Dedicated `{% cv_cancel_url %}` tag without `@ignore_exception` suppression**: Unlike `cv_context_url` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:281-292`), which is decorated with `@ignore_exception(ViewSetKeyFoundError, default_value=None)` and calls `cv_get_context` (which would emit a new origin link from the current view), `cv_cancel_url` delegates directly to `view.cv_get_cancel_url()` so developer `ImproperlyConfigured` errors surface while inaccessible destinations cleanly return `None`.
- **No recapture of intermediate selector view**: Forwarding the validated capture in `PolymorphicCreateSelectView.form_valid()` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:68-72`) rather than calling `cv_get_link_url()` preserves the original `"list"`/`"card"` origin and query state while keeping `kwargs = {"polymorphic_ctype_id": polymorphic_ctype_id}` intact.

### Concerns
- **[HIGH] Required property — Task `03-01`'s `<verify>` command (`pytest tests/test1/test_cancel_polymorphic.py tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py`) must pass (CAN-04, CAN-07)**:
  - **Evidence**: `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py:46,170-176` and `tests/test1/conftest.py:220-228,255-258`.
  - **Mechanism**: Task `03-01` runs `pytest tests/test1/test_cancel_polymorphic.py tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py -n 2 -q`. In `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py:46`, `library_delete_case` for `family == "author"` logs in `client_user_author_delete`, which only has `"delete"` permission (`tests/test1/conftest.py:220-228`) and lacks `"view"` permission on `cv_author`. At `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py:174`, `_exercise_actual_library_delete` asserts `_golden_bytes(_extract_controls(content, ("delete", "reset"))) == (GOLDEN_DIR / "delete.html").read_bytes()`. Because `AuthorListView.cv_has_access(user_author_delete)` is `False`, Plan `06.7-01` omits the `"reset"` (Cancel) button on `/author/{pk}/delete/`, causing `_extract_controls(content, ("delete", "reset"))` (`tests/test1/test_form_buttons_golden.py:64`) to fail with `AssertionError: {'delete': 1, 'reset': 0}`! Yet `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py` is not in `files_modified` for Plan `06.7-01` or `06.7-03`.
- **[MEDIUM] Required property — Tampered cancel tokens on `PolymorphicCreateSelectView` must not degrade into trusted key-only cancel origins on `PolymorphicCreateView` when `cv_success_keys` is also configured (CAN-02, CAN-04, D-08, D-11)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:68-72`, `06.7-01-PLAN.md` (`<interfaces>`), and `06.7-03-PLAN.md` (Task `03-01`).
  - **Mechanism**: Plan `06.7-01` `<interfaces>` specifies: *"a present but invalid/oversized token forces configured fallback and never degrades into a trusted key-only origin. A missing token may use the existing validated key-only origin behavior"*. Meanwhile, Plan `06.7-03` Task `03-01` specifies: *"When capture is present and invalid, drop it and the cancel-origin key together; any key independently needed for unchanged success behavior follows the existing success allow-list validation."* Because `cv_from` is a single shared query parameter (`CRUD_VIEWS_ORIGIN_PARAM`), if `PolymorphicCreateView` has both `cv_cancel_keys = "auto"` and `cv_success_keys = ["list"]`, and a request to `create_select` has `?cv_from=list&cv_from_cancel=INVALID`:
    - If `create_select.form_valid()` keeps `cv_from=list` (for `cv_success_keys`) and drops `cv_from_cancel=INVALID`, then the redirected `GET /vehicle/create/ct/1/?cv_from=list` arrives at `PolymorphicCreateView` with a **missing** `cv_from_cancel` token, so `PolymorphicCreateView` will treat `?cv_from=list` as a valid legacy key-only cancel origin (violating *"never degrades into a trusted key-only origin"*).
    - Conversely, if `create_select.form_valid()` drops `cv_from=list` too, then `PolymorphicCreateView` loses its `cv_success_keys` origin (violating *"any key independently needed for unchanged success behavior follows the existing success allow-list validation"*).
- **[MEDIUM] Required property — `PolymorphicCreateSelectView` link emission must respect `PolymorphicCreateView`'s `cv_success_keys` / `cv_cancel_keys` when `create_select` is the entry link on list/card pages (CAN-04, D-08, D-15)**:
  - **Evidence**: `tests/test1/app/views.py:531` (`cv_context_actions = ["create_select"]`), `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:790-815`, and `src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:24-36`.
  - **Mechanism**: On a polymorphic ViewSet (`cv_vehicle`), the list toolbar links to `"create_select"` (`PolymorphicCreateSelectView`), not `"create"` (`PolymorphicCreateView`). `cv_get_link_origin_key(cls)` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:790-806`) inspects `cls.cv_cancel_keys` and `cls.cv_success_keys` on `PolymorphicCreateSelectView`. If a developer configures `cv_success_keys = ["list"]` or `cv_cancel_keys = None` on `PolymorphicCreateView` without also setting it on `PolymorphicCreateSelectView`, `cv_get_link_origin_key(PolymorphicCreateSelectView)` only sees `PolymorphicCreateSelectView`'s attributes.
- **[LOW] Required property — `cv_cancel_url` template tag must handle empty-string `obj=""` arguments from templates safely (CAN-06, D-12)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:242,255,273,284`.
  - **Mechanism**: Every other object-accepting tag in `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py` (`cv_context_action`, `cv_context_action_form`, `cv_context_button`, `cv_context_url`) begins with `obj = obj if obj else None  # fix empty string from template`. If `cv_cancel_url(context, obj=None)` only checks `if obj is None:`, a template passing an unpopulated variable (`obj=""`) will bypass `getattr(view, "object", None)` and fail when resolving `obj.pk`.

### Suggestions
- Include `obj = obj if obj else None` in `cv_cancel_url(context, obj=None)` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py`), matching `cv_context_url` at line 284.
- To resolve the invalid-token forwarding ambiguity in `PolymorphicCreateSelectView.form_valid()`, when `cv_from_cancel` is present in `self.request.GET` and invalid, drop both `cv_from` and `cv_from_cancel` unless `cv_from` is only used for `cv_success_keys`; if `cv_from` must be retained for `cv_success_keys` on `create`, either forward the invalid `cv_from_cancel` marker (or an explicit invalid/suppressed cancel marker) so `PolymorphicCreateView` knows cancel origin was invalidated while `cv_get_success_key()` still reads `cv_from`.
- Grant `"view"` permission to the delete test user in `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py:46` (or update the fixture in Plan `06.7-01`) before running Task `03-01`'s verification command.

### Risk Assessment
**MEDIUM** — The template tag and selector forwarding are localized to two files, but the `test_polymorphic.py:174` failure and the invalid-token forwarding edge case between `cv_cancel_keys` and `cv_success_keys` need to be addressed before execution.

## 06.7-04

### Summary
Plan `06.7-04` updates reference documentation (`docs/reference/update_view.md`, `create_view.md`, `delete_view.md`, `custom_form_view.md`, `settings.md`), `docs/migrating-1.0.md`, `CHANGELOG.md`, and `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py`, accompanied by executable doc tests in `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py`. Source verification against `docs/` and `examples/bootstrap5/` uncovers a direct sync-gate break: removing `cv_cancel_keys = ["list", "detail"]` from `AuthorUpdateView` in `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py:97` without updating `docs/faq.md:221-229` will break `examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py:87`, and existing example tests in `examples/bootstrap5/library/tests.py:69,77` and `examples/bootstrap5/context_buttons/tests.py:52` assert exact `?cv_from=detail"` / `?cv_from=list"` substrings without `cv_from_cancel`.

### Strengths
- **Executable documentation tests (`tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py`)**: Following the pattern established in `tests/test1/test_form_button_docs.py:38-49,168-194`, extracting and rendering the actual `{% cv_cancel_url as url %}{% if url %}...{% endif %}` template fence from the markdown files guarantees that documented recipes cannot drift from runtime behavior.
- **Paired `CHANGELOG.md` and `docs/migrating-1.0.md` breaking entries**: Adheres to the repository mandate (`AGENTS.md:107`) that every `### Breaking` entry in `CHANGELOG.md` has a corresponding migration section in `docs/migrating-1.0.md`.

### Concerns
- **[HIGH] Required property — Modifying `AuthorUpdateView` in `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py` must not break `<!-- cv-sync: library/views.py -->` blocks in other documentation files (CAN-06, D-14)**:
  - **Evidence**: `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py:93-107`, `docs/faq.md:215-252`, `docs/reference/update_view.md:62-70`, and `examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py:21-24,86-92`.
  - **Mechanism**: Task `04-03` modifies `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py` and `docs/reference/update_view.md` to remove `cv_cancel_keys = ["list", "detail"]` from `AuthorUpdateView` (`library/views.py:97`). However, `docs/faq.md:221-229` **also** has a `<!-- cv-sync: library/views.py -->` marker containing the exact 6-line `AuthorUpdateView` definition including `cv_cancel_keys = ["list", "detail"]`, and `docs/faq.md:217,247` still states *"Form views go back to the list by default, both on Cancel (`cv_cancel_key`)..."* and *"Returning to the list drops its filter, sort order and page."* Because `docs/faq.md` is not in `06.7-04-PLAN.md`'s `files_modified`, `examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py:87` (`test_marked_block_matches_source[faq.md:library/views.py]`) will fail, and `docs/faq.md` will contradict `docs/reference/update_view.md`. Furthermore, `AuthorDeleteView` in `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py:106`, `TicketUpdateView` and `TicketDeleteView` in `examples/bootstrap5/context_buttons/views.py:84,91`, and the description in `examples/bootstrap5/project/features.py:81` still reference explicit `cv_cancel_keys = ["list", "detail"]`.
- **[HIGH] Required property — Example app test suites in `examples/bootstrap5/` must remain green after link emission adds `cv_from_cancel` and list restoration adds `cv_from_restore=1` (CAN-02, CAN-06, D-14)**:
  - **Evidence**: `examples/bootstrap5/library/tests.py:65-80`, `examples/bootstrap5/context_buttons/tests.py:50-54`, and `noxfile.py:117-129`.
  - **Mechanism**:
    1. `examples/bootstrap5/library/tests.py:69` asserts `self.assertContains(resp, f'href="{update_url}?cv_from=detail"')` (with a closing quote `"` immediately after `detail`), and line 77 asserts `self.assertContains(resp, f'href="{update_url}?cv_from=list"')` (with a closing quote `"` immediately after `list`). Once `cv_get_link_url()` appends `&cv_from_cancel=<token>`, both assertions fail.
    2. `examples/bootstrap5/context_buttons/tests.py:52` asserts `tile = f'href="{self.update_url}?cv_from=detail" class="card text-decoration-none" id="url-tile"'` and `self.assertIn(tile, self.rendered(resp))`. Because `ticket_detail.html` uses `{% cv_context_url "update" as edit_url %}`, which calls `cv_get_link_url()` and emits `?cv_from=detail&amp;cv_from_cancel=<token>`, this assertion also fails.
    Neither file is listed in `06.7-04-PLAN.md`'s `files_modified`.
- **[MEDIUM] Required property — Documentation must reference a valid, non-colliding system check ID (CAN-05, D-09, D-14)**:
  - **Evidence**: `06.7-04-PLAN.md` Tasks `04-01` and `04-03`, `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392`, and `docs/migrating-1.0.md:34-42`.
  - **Mechanism**: Tasks `04-01` and `04-03` document `viewset.E255` as the missing-cancel-fallback startup check and hand `E255` to Phase 7's stability inventory, colliding with the existing `viewset.E255` check for `ContextButton` targets (`src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392`). Additionally, `docs/reference/card-list-view.md:314-316` discusses `cv_cancel_keys` on card views and should be checked for consistency with the new `"auto"` default.

### Suggestions
- Add `docs/faq.md`, `examples/bootstrap5/library/tests.py`, `examples/bootstrap5/context_buttons/tests.py`, `examples/bootstrap5/context_buttons/views.py`, and `examples/bootstrap5/project/features.py` to `06.7-04-PLAN.md`'s `files_modified`.
- Add `PYTHONPATH=src .nox/tests-3-12-django-5-2/bin/python -m pytest examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py -q` (and the `examples/bootstrap5` test suite) to Task `04-03`'s `<verify>` block so `cv-sync` drift is caught immediately in Wave 3.
- Replace `viewset.E255` with `viewset.E256` (or `viewset.E265`) in all reference docs, `docs/migrating-1.0.md`, and `tests/test1/test_cancel_docs.py`.

### Risk Assessment
**MEDIUM** — Documentation and example updates are straightforward, but omitting `docs/faq.md` and `examples/bootstrap5/*/tests.py` guarantees failures in `test_docs_sync.py` and the `examples` test session.

## 06.7-05

### Summary
Plan `06.7-05` adds a real-browser Playwright/Chromium acceptance harness (`tests/browser/cancel-navigation.cjs`, `tests/browser/cancel_navigation_server.py`, `tests/browser/cancel-navigation.test.cjs`) and executes the full multi-version verification gates to produce `.planning/phases/06.7-dynamic-cancel-navigation-and-origin-query-state/06.7-ACCEPTANCE.md`. While the owned-process browser harness design matches existing harnesses in `tests/browser/`, source verification shows that Task `05-02`'s verification gates will fail on multiple existing test files (`test_theme_form_requests.py`, `test_form_buttons_golden.py`, `test_action_menus_golden.py`, `test_action_menus_list.py`, `test_polymorphic.py`) that are broken by permission-aware Cancel omission (D-10) and automatic cancel defaults (D-02) but are not included in `files_modified` of any plan.

### Strengths
- **Owned-process browser verification with source-hash and import binding**: Following `tests/browser/presentation.cjs` (`lines 1-65`) and `tests/browser/modal-focus.cjs` (`lines 68-105`), validating the resolved `crud_views` import path, runtime versions, and non-write network/database invariants in a real browser directly verifies D-01, D-06, D-07, D-08, D-10, and D-12.
- **Three-series Django matrix with explicit `PYTHONPATH=src`**: Running Django 5.2, 4.2, and the 6.0 `crispy_floor` environment with `PYTHONPATH=src` avoids the stale installed-package pitfall identified during research while keeping only the single documented `test_filter_form_baseline` exclusion (`noxfile.py:65`).

### Concerns
- **[HIGH] Required property — Task `05-02`'s first `<verify>` gate (`test_theme_form_requests.py`) and full pytest gates (`test_form_buttons_golden.py`, `test_polymorphic.py`, `test_action_menus_golden.py`, `test_action_menus_list.py`) must pass without modifying undeclared files (CAN-07, D-10, D-14, D-15)**:
  - **Evidence**:
    1. `tests/test1/test_theme_form_requests.py:79-108` and `tests/test1/conftest.py:210-217,239-246,663-668`.
    2. `tests/test1/test_form_buttons_golden.py:64,119-135,149-190,409-460`.
    3. `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py:46,174`.
    4. `tests/test1/test_action_menus_golden.py:105-143` and `tests/test1/test_action_menus_list.py:61`.
  - **Mechanism**:
    1. In `tests/test1/test_theme_form_requests.py:89-98`, `test_owned_form_request_matrix` runs with `user_campaign_change` (for `workflow`), `user_author_add` (for `create`), and `user_author_change` (for `update`). None of these three users has `"view"` permission (`conftest.py:210-246,663-668`). Under D-10, `AuthorListView.cv_has_access` and `CampaignListView.cv_has_access` return `False`, so the Cancel button is omitted. Then `_owned()` at `test_theme_form_requests.py:79` executes `cancel = form.cssselect("[data-cv-cancel-url]")[0]`, raising `IndexError: list index out of range`!
    2. In `tests/test1/test_form_buttons_golden.py:119-135`, all four `CONTROL_CASES` (`create`, `update`, `delete`, `workflow`) use single-permission clients (`client_user_author_add`, `client_user_author_change`, `client_user_author_delete`, `client_user_campaign_change`) that lack `"view"` permission. Under D-10, Cancel (`"reset"`) is omitted on all four pages, breaking `_extract_controls(..., ("submit", "reset"))` (`AssertionError: {'submit': 1, 'reset': 0}`) and `_form_cancel_link()` (`AssertionError: 0 cancel links in the owning form`) across the entire file!
    3. In `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py:46,174`, `client_user_author_delete` lacks `"view"` permission, failing `_extract_controls(content, ("delete", "reset"))`.
    4. In `tests/test1/test_action_menus_golden.py:105-143` and `tests/test1/test_action_menus_list.py:61`, `AuthorCreateView`, `AuthorUpdateView`, `AuthorDeleteView`, and `PublisherCreateView` now default to `cv_cancel_keys = "auto"`, appending `?cv_from=...&cv_from_cancel=...` to action `href`s and breaking `assert update.get("href").endswith("/update/")` as well as `list_full.html`, `toolbar_detail_full.html`, `toolbar_publisher_list.html`, and `cards_author_full.html`.
    5. Task `05-02`'s `<files>` only lists `.planning/phases/06.7-dynamic-cancel-navigation-and-origin-query-state/06.7-ACCEPTANCE.md`, while instructing the executor to *"repair that exact assertion with explicit static configuration"* if a fixture assertion fails — creating a mismatch between `files_modified` and the actual test files that must be updated (which belong in Plans `06.7-01` / `06.7-03`).
- **[MEDIUM] Required property — Browser server (`tests/browser/cancel_navigation_server.py`) must not depend on external CDN fetches or conflict with reserved ports `8143`, `8144`, `8145` (CAN-07)**:
  - **Evidence**: `tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:14-30`, `tests/browser/presentation.cjs:2-6`, `tests/browser/theme-adoption.cjs:45-47`, and `tests/browser/modal-focus.cjs:21,79`.
  - **Mechanism**: Existing browser harnesses reserve ports `8143`, `8144`, and `8145`, and `tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:14-30` loads Bootstrap, Font Awesome, and jQuery from external CDNs (`cdn.jsdelivr.net`, `cdnjs.cloudflare.com`, `code.jquery.com`) with SRI hashes. If `cancel_navigation_server.py` uses `app/base.html` without local asset routing or if `cancel-navigation.cjs` does not bind an ephemeral port (`0`) or a dedicated non-conflicting port (e.g., `8146` with port validation), headless Chromium runs in restricted/offline environments or concurrent runs will fail.
- **[LOW] Required property — Final lint/format and example verification gates should cover `examples/` (CAN-06, CAN-07, D-14)**:
  - **Evidence**: `06.7-05-PLAN.md` Task `05-02` (`<verify>`) and `tests/browser/theme_adoption_sources.py:1367-1368,1408`.
  - **Mechanism**: Task `05-02` runs `.venv/bin/ruff check src tests` and `.venv/bin/ruff format --check src tests`, omitting `examples` even though Plan `06.7-04` modifies `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py`, and does not run `pytest -c examples/bootstrap5/pytest.ini examples/bootstrap5` (which runs `examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py` and the example app tests).

### Suggestions
- Move the necessary fixture/test updates for `tests/test1/test_form_buttons_golden.py`, `tests/test1/test_theme_form_requests.py`, `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py`, `tests/test1/test_action_menus_golden.py`, and `tests/test1/test_action_menus_list.py` into Plan `06.7-01` (or explicitly list them in `files_modified` where those contracts change) rather than discovering them during `06.7-05` Task `05-02`:
  - In `test_form_buttons_golden.py`, `test_theme_form_requests.py`, and `test_polymorphic.py`, grant `"view"` permission to the form test users (or grant `"view"` in a local fixture) so the fallback `"list"` target remains accessible and the Cancel button renders as before, while testing permission-denied Cancel omission in `test_cancel_permissions.py`.
  - In `test_action_menus_golden.py` and `test_action_menus_list.py`, either set `cv_cancel_keys = None` on the fixture views whose purpose is testing static action-menu markup (preserving `toolbar_publisher_list.html` byte-identity per `ROADMAP.md` Phase 3 Success Criterion 1) or update the path assertion in `test_action_menus_list.py:61` to check `urlsplit(update.get("href")).path.endswith("/update/")`.
- Expand Task `05-02`'s `<verify>` block to include `.venv/bin/ruff check src tests examples`, `.venv/bin/ruff format --check src tests examples`, and `PYTHONPATH=src .nox/tests-3-12-django-5-2/bin/python -m pytest -c examples/bootstrap5/pytest.ini examples/bootstrap5 -q`.
- Ensure `tests/browser/cancel_navigation_server.py` serves local/self-contained templates and static assets (like `tests/browser/conditional_formsets_server.py`) and validates its port (defaulting to `0` or `8146`, rejecting `8143`, `8144`, `8145`).

### Risk Assessment
**HIGH** — Task `05-02` will fail on at least 5 existing test modules (`test_theme_form_requests.py`, `test_form_buttons_golden.py`, `test_polymorphic.py`, `test_action_menus_golden.py`, `test_action_menus_list.py`) unless those fixture interactions with D-02 (`cv_cancel_keys = "auto"`) and D-10 (permission-aware Cancel omission) are resolved in earlier waves.

## Cross-Plan Comparison & Overall Assessment

### Cross-Plan Dependency & Conflict Summary
1. **Check ID Collision (`06.7-02` → `06.7-04` → Phase 7)**: Plan `06.7-02` defines `viewset.E255` for missing cancel fallbacks and Plan `06.7-04` documents it for Phase 7's stability freeze, overlooking that `viewset.E255` is already defined in `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392` and tested in `tests/test1/test_context_button_check.py:9`.
2. **Wave 1 Permission Omission Cascade (`06.7-01` → `06.7-03` → `06.7-05`)**: Plan `06.7-01` implements permission-aware Cancel omission (`_cv_cancel_target_available` + `get_cancel_button()` returning `None`). In `tests/test1/conftest.py`, the standard form test fixtures (`user_author_add`, `user_author_change`, `user_author_delete`, `user_publisher_change`, `user_campaign_change`) only grant the single write permission (`add`/`change`/`delete`) and do **not** grant `"view"` permission. Because `AuthorListView`, `PublisherListView`, and `CampaignListView` require `"view"` permission, Cancel is omitted for all of those fixtures, immediately breaking `test_cancel_origin.py:47-61` (in `06.7-01`), `test_polymorphic.py:174` (in `06.7-03`), and `test_theme_form_requests.py:79` + `test_form_buttons_golden.py` (in `06.7-05`).
3. **Wave 1 Auto-Default Link Emission Cascade (`06.7-01` → `06.7-04` → `06.7-05`)**: Setting `CrudViewProcessFormMixin.cv_cancel_keys = "auto"` in `06.7-01` causes `cv_get_link_url()` to append `?cv_from=...&cv_from_cancel=...` on `create`, `update`, and `delete` links across `cv_author`, `cv_publisher`, and the example apps, breaking `test_action_menus_golden.py`, `test_action_menus_list.py:61`, `examples/bootstrap5/library/tests.py:69,77`, and `examples/bootstrap5/context_buttons/tests.py:52`.
4. **Docs Sync Break (`06.7-04`)**: Editing `AuthorUpdateView` in `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py:97` without updating the matching `<!-- cv-sync: library/views.py -->` block in `docs/faq.md:221-229` breaks `examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py:87`.

### Overall Risk Assessment
**HIGH** — The architectural design for Phase `06.7` satisfies all seven `CAN-01`–`CAN-07` requirements and decisions `D-01`–`D-16`, but the five plans must be revised before execution to:
1. Replace colliding check ID `viewset.E255` with `viewset.E256` (or `viewset.E265`).
2. Clean `cv_from_restore=1` and navigation parameters on all `ListView` and `CardListView` requests (not only inside `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get`) and rebind `request.GET` as well as `request.META["QUERY_STRING"]`.
3. Fix Guardian's `_cv_cancel_target_available` for `AnonymousUser` (`user.is_authenticated`), single-invocation `cv_action_enabled`, and nested child views.
4. Explicitly account for existing single-permission test fixtures, action-menu goldens, `docs/faq.md`, and `examples/bootstrap5` tests in the plan `files_modified` lists.


---

## Consensus Summary

### Agreed Strengths

All three reviewers support sibling-only reversal, independent signed query capture, unchanged success redirects, the dedicated URL helper, and permission-aware omission before crispy action-group construction. The selected MRO location for automatic form defaults and explicit selector forwarding match existing source.

### Agreed Concerns

- R1: E255 already belongs to context-button checks (all three reviewers).
- R2: restoration cleanup must cover ordinary list/card views without the filter-persistence mixin (all three).
- R3/R4: the default and omission changes affect existing golden, URL and single-permission fixtures outside the implementation manifests (overlapping Fable/Grok/Gemini findings).
- R12/R13: documentation and example-suite scope is incomplete; FAQ sync and existing exact example links need ownership and verification (Grok/Gemini, with Fable identifying the buttons reference gap).

### Divergent Views and adjudication

- Grok calls raw `"auto"` iteration a HIGH. Existing code does iterate the old list field, but Plan01 explicitly creates `effective_cancel_keys`, changes E252 to accept auto/None/list, updates `cv_get_cancel_key` for effective defaults, and wires the emitter through the new contract. Fable specifically credits these planned adaptations. This is already incorporated, not an unresolved HIGH merely because the old code has not yet been changed. Executors must still implement and test those adapters.
- Gemini’s unsaved-object fallback HIGH is already covered by Plan01’s missing/impossible-fallback error and saved-object contract plus Plan02’s explicit runtime error for an unusable configured target. Its added suggestion should be used as a concrete test, not treated as an absent requirement.
- Gemini’s anonymous-user and repeated-state-hook concerns are already explicit in Plan02-02 (anonymous policy and “Call action state once”). The distinct parent-only target/MRO gap is retained as R16.
- Gemini’s preferred startup policy (always reject explicit invalid fallback even with an available origin) differs from the recorded plan’s deliberate lazy-fallback policy. D-09 leaves timing to planning and Plan02 explicitly requires a runtime error whenever fallback is needed; this alternative is advisory, not an additional forced behavior change.
- Fable’s same-wave repair concern is retained as INFO R9, consistent with temporal-coupling advisory treatment. Declared Plan02/03 writes are disjoint; exceptional repair sequencing must respect ownership.

### Findings requiring executable plan disposition

These are deduplicated, unresolved findings. Required properties and evidence are binding; reviewer suggestions above are examples only. A revised plan can implement a smaller correct mechanism or explicitly defer/reject an actionable non-HIGH with rationale. Merely acknowledging a finding in this report does not resolve it. No PLAN was edited during review.

#### R1 — HIGH — Plan 02,04

**Required property:** The new fallback diagnostic has a unique check identifier and the same identifier is used throughout executable tasks and documentation.

**Evidence:** Plan 02 assigns E255 and claims it was absent; src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392 already emits E255 for context-button targets. Plan 04 repeats E255.

**Reviewers:** fable-review, grok-review, gemini-review.

#### R2 — HIGH — Plan 01

**Required property:** All accepted list/card return destinations consume internal restoration fields before producing subsequent navigation controls.

**Evidence:** Plan 01 appends restore=1 to both list and card captures but assigns consumption only to ListViewTableFilterMixin.get. CardListView at src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17 does not use that mixin; order controls copy query fields at views/mixins.py:271-279 and pagination at templatetags/crud_views.py:557-569.

**Reviewers:** fable-review, grok-review, gemini-review.

#### R3 — HIGH — Plan 01,05

**Required property:** The approved dynamic-default change has an explicit, owned and verifiable policy for affected committed action-link goldens.

**Evidence:** Four tests/test1/golden/action_menus snapshots contain links to forms whose defaults change. Plan 01 owns no golden updates or normalization; Plan 05 forbids blind regeneration and owns only final evidence. The full suite therefore has no planned owner for intentional golden differences.

**Reviewers:** fable-review, gemini-review.

#### R4 — HIGH — Plan 01,02,05

**Required property:** Existing permission-sensitive and exact-link regression fixtures are explicitly migrated and verified in the implementation wave that changes their behavior.

**Evidence:** tests/test1/test_guardian_parent_none.py:875 compares a card-generated create URL exactly. Similar assertions exist in authorization/template/action-group tests outside Plan 01's test ownership; Plan 02 runs the affected file and Plan 05 discovers the rest. Additionally test_theme_form_requests.py:79 indexes a Cancel control for change-only/add-only users; test_form_buttons_golden.py:119–135 and test_polymorphic.py:174 require Cancel for users without view permission. New permission omission intentionally removes it. These files have no implementation owner.

**Reviewers:** fable-review, gemini-review.

#### R5 — MEDIUM — Plan 01

**Required property:** The tracer provides a bounded early integration checkpoint whose failures are diagnosable within its task scope, or the plan explicitly justifies the larger atomic slice.

**Evidence:** 01-01 requires the complete bounded parser, authorization adapter, crispy omission, link emission and list/session restoration before its single tracer gate.

**Reviewers:** fable-review.

#### R6 — LOW — Plan 01,04

**Required property:** The documented transport limits distinguish library parsing bounds from deployment request-line limits.

**Evidence:** Plan 01 permits 16384-byte tokens but docs tasks discuss only library limits; URLs accepted by the library may be rejected by an application's proxy/server before Django receives them.

**Reviewers:** fable-review.

#### R7 — LOW — Plan 04

**Required property:** Public fallback documentation explains the retained legal cv_cancel_key=None behavior.

**Evidence:** Plan 01 preserves current-view resolution, matching src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:501-502, but Plan 04 documents cv_cancel_keys=None without explicitly distinguishing the singular None fallback.

**Reviewers:** fable-review.

#### R8 — MEDIUM — Plan 02

**Required property:** The new startup diagnostic is exercised against the existing registered form configurations and intentional invalid fixtures have an explicit disposition.

**Evidence:** 02-01 introduces a startup error for missing fallbacks but covers new diagnostic cases only; existing form-only registered fixtures are not inventoried. D-09 requires an error, so changing it to a warning is not an admissible remedy.

**Reviewers:** fable-review.

#### R9 — INFO — Plan 02,03

**Required property:** A dependency defect found by Plan 03 cannot trigger concurrent writes to the base.py file owned by active Plan 02.

**Evidence:** 03-02 says core defects are repaired in their owning plan while same-wave 02 modifies base.py; no sequencing rule covers this repair path.

**Reviewers:** fable-review.

#### R11 — LOW — Plan 04

**Required property:** Signed-capture documentation explains invalidation and safe fallback across signing-key rotation, or explicitly defers this operational guidance.

**Evidence:** Plan 04 documents signed URL visibility and malformed-input fallback but has no signing-key rotation guidance.

**Reviewers:** fable-review.

#### R12 — HIGH — Plan 04

**Required property:** Every existing published cancel explanation and synchronized example affected by the new default is updated together.

**Evidence:** docs/faq.md:221–229 copies AuthorUpdateView under cv-sync and :247 says list state is lost. Plan04 removes that class attribute without owning FAQ. docs/reference/card-list-view.md:314–317 and examples/bootstrap5/project/features.py:81–82 retain explicit-key-only explanations; docs/reference/buttons.md:617–620 omits URL-or-None. These files are outside planned ownership.

**Reviewers:** grok-review, fable-review, gemini-review.

#### R13 — HIGH — Plan 04,05

**Required property:** The updated example and synchronized documentation are verified by the example app tests as well as package tests.

**Evidence:** examples/bootstrap5/library/tests.py:65–79 asserts exact key-only hrefs; examples/bootstrap5/test_docs_sync.py verifies every marked fence. Plans04/05 run package tests and MkDocs, neither of which exercises these example suites. examples/bootstrap5/context_buttons/tests.py:52 has another exact key-only link assertion. Changed example files also fall outside the final Ruff scope.

**Reviewers:** grok-review, gemini-review.

#### R14 — MEDIUM — Plan 03,04

**Required property:** Custom form action-group/helper integrations have a tested and documented path for get_cancel_button returning None.

**Evidence:** src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py:138–149 inlines get_cancel_button in FormActions. The plan filters core groups and tests kwargs hooks, but does not exercise a custom get_form_actions/helper subclass adapting the changed return contract.

**Reviewers:** grok-review.

#### R15 — LOW — Plan 02

**Required property:** Explicit custom cancel destinations preserve the existing target rendering/state-hook contract or explicitly justify the supported restriction.

**Evidence:** CreateView._cv_action_enabled_for_rendering at src/crud_views/lib/views/create.py:51–70 adapts target context; the proposed core availability hook calls raw cv_action_enabled. Plan02 promises custom keys but has no explicit custom-create/render-hook agreement case.

**Reviewers:** grok-review.

#### R16 — HIGH — Plan 02

**Required property:** Guardian parent-only/custom no-object targets use their destination parent authorization rather than an inapplicable child/model permission fallback.

**Evidence:** GuardianParentPermissionMixin.has_permission at src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:262–265 deliberately bypasses model-level permission for nested targets. GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired.cv_has_access at src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:381–382 denies None. The plan combines target cv_has_access(user,None) and super adaptation but has no explicit parent-only target branch/test. Anonymous denial and single state evaluation are already specified, and are not additional gaps.

**Reviewers:** gemini-review.

#### R17 — MEDIUM — Plan 01,02

**Required property:** Expected missing-parent outcomes in core target resolution are deliberate and tested without swallowing programming or custom-hook failures.

**Evidence:** src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:864–876 performs a parent lookup that can raise Http404. Plan02 gives the Guardian path an expected-Http404 policy; Plan01 core target availability has no explicit counterpart. Blanket KeyError catching suggested by the reviewer is not mandatory and must not hide malformed developer context.

**Reviewers:** gemini-review.

#### R18 — MEDIUM — Plan 01,03

**Required property:** An invalid cancel capture cannot become a trusted absent-token legacy origin during selector forwarding while the shared success key is retained.

**Evidence:** Plan01 interface distinguishes present invalid token from absent token. Plan03-01 drops the invalid token but permits retaining cv_from for cv_success_keys; the concrete create then receives a valid key with no token and accepts legacy cancel origin. Both policies cannot hold without preserving cancel invalidity separately.

**Reviewers:** gemini-review.

#### R19 — MEDIUM — Plan 03

**Required property:** Selector and concrete-create combinations of automatic/static cancel and independent success configuration have an explicit tested contract.

**Evidence:** Entry links inspect PolymorphicCreateSelectView attributes at base.py:790–815, while selected concrete create may have different cv_cancel_keys/cv_success_keys. Plan03 validates the concrete cancel allow-list but does not specify the complete independent-override matrix. Existing success behavior must not be expanded inadvertently.

**Reviewers:** gemini-review.

#### R20 — LOW — Plan 03

**Required property:** The public cancel template tag handles unresolved/empty object arguments consistently with the established object-accepting tag contract.

**Evidence:** src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:242,255,273,284 normalizes empty template values to None. The new tag task specifies obj=None but omits an unresolved variable/empty-string case.

**Reviewers:** gemini-review.

#### R21 — MEDIUM — Plan 05

**Required property:** Browser acceptance has a declared dependency on available local assets or records an explicit prerequisite instead of silently depending on external CDN availability.

**Evidence:** tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:14–30 loads Bootstrap/jQuery and icon assets from CDNs; plan05 specifies isolated current-source server and cached browser tools but not asset provisioning. Modal/button interaction evidence must exercise the actual loaded scripts. Its available-port requirement already addresses port conflicts.

**Reviewers:** gemini-review.

### Other observations already covered or advisory

- Disabled-persistence cleaning and rebinding both request.GET and META are expressly required in Plan01-01; cleanup/session cases are in 01-03. The non-filter destination gap is separately R2.
- No-object tag rendering, repeated invalid submissions, following the selector redirect, decoded multivalue comparisons and protected-delete omission are expressly required in Plan03. These reviewer reminders add no missing task.
- Automatic selector defaults use the same general representation explicitly frozen in Plan01/03. Reference attribute tables expressly change to auto/None/list in Plan04.
- Parent resolution is explicitly nested-only; unexpected hook/programming errors propagate. Do not apply a suggestion to catch all KeyError/AssertionError as an omission shortcut. Expected missing-parent treatment is R17.
- The filtered `-k` check is followed by a full new permission-module run in Plan02-02, so naming alone cannot permanently hide a case.
- Plan05 already requires an available loopback port, explicit missing-runtime failure, current-source imports, owned cleanup, and real clicked navigation. Reusing an owned-process pattern does not authorize copying companion-specific roots/runtimes. Runtime discovery remains execution work, not a verified review precondition.
- Plan05’s static-fixture exception is explicitly limited to a static-purpose fixture; Plan01 forbids blanket shared-fixture opt-outs. Those policies are not contradictory. The known fixture migrations still need ownership (R3/R4).
- Request-local parent-query caching is a performance suggestion, not a demonstrated goal failure. No extra query budget is approved. Companion acceptance remains outside this phase; the review does not claim it was rerun.
- Fable’s unspecific getting-started tutorial-copy concern was not confirmed by source search; the actual stale copies are captured in R12.

### Consensus gate audit

Three real, source-cited reviews engage the multi-reviewer gate. R1 and R12 are existence-class claims independently confirmed in source (and corroborated). R2/R3/R4/R13 are correctness/coverage judgments with overlapping reviewer support. R16 is a single-reviewer judgment from an unmarked, grounded reviewer, so it counts without requiring corroboration. No surviving HIGH is suppressed as single-reviewer/unconfirmed. Existing-plan coverage exclusions above are not consensus suppression. Cross-artifact drift and UNCHECKABLE coverage advisories do not enter unresolved counts.

## Claude Review (fable-review) — cycle 2

# Cycle 2 re-review: Phase 06.7 plans at `dfd98c2`

Scope and method: the five on-disk plans were re-read at the revision commit and compared against the working tree. All claims below were checked against source with read-only greps and file reads. No tests were run, no agents were spawned, nothing was written. Browser runtime availability (Playwright/Chromium cache) was not inspected and remains unverified by this review.

Round 1 sign-off summary for the HIGH findings is given first, then each plan.

| Finding | Severity | Verdict | Owner | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| R1 E255 collision | HIGH | FULLY RESOLVED | 02, 04 | `E256` has no declaration anywhere under `src`, `tests` or `docs` (grep empty); `E250`–`E255` and `E260`–`E264` are the taken ids. 02-01 reserves E256 with a uniqueness test and keeps `tests/test1/test_context_button_check.py` for E255. 04-01/04-03 publish E256. |
| R2 card/list restore consumers | HIGH | FULLY RESOLVED | 01 | Cleanup moved to `CrudView.dispatch` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:227`), which is the only core dispatch; `list.py`/`card.py` override neither `dispatch` nor `get`, and `ListViewTableFilterMixin.get` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:357`) runs from `View.dispatch` afterwards. `CardListView(CardOrderMixin, CrudView, generic.ListView)` at `card.py:17` is covered; the order form (`mixins.py:272-279`), `cv_pagination` (`templatetags/crud_views.py:558-563`) and `cv_config` QUERY_STRING (`:103-111`) all read after cleanup. `request.GET` is a `cached_property` (`django/core/handlers/wsgi.py:85-89`), so rebinding is valid. 01-03 replays plain-list, plain-card and filtered-card controls. |
| R3 golden policy | HIGH | FULLY RESOLVED | 01, 05 | Exactly four of the eight action-menu goldens contain form links (`list_full`, `toolbar_detail_full`, `cards_author_full`, `toolbar_publisher_list`); the disabled variants and `cards_publisher_child` link only to detail/list/manage. 01-02 names precisely those four, validates route/origin/token before normalizing only navigation parameters, and forbids regeneration. 05-02 consumes that policy. |
| R4 fixture migration | HIGH | FULLY RESOLVED | 01, 02, 05 | All twelve modules in 01-02's file list exist and are in its second verify command. The permission mechanism is correct: `test_theme_form_requests.py:79` indexes `[data-cv-cancel-url]` for `user_campaign_change`/`user_author_add`/`user_author_change` (`conftest.py:663`, `:239`, `:210`), which hold only their write permission; `test_form_buttons_golden.py:119-135` uses the same single-permission clients; `test_polymorphic.py:171-176` reuses the delete golden. 01-02 grants view only to Cancel-present fixtures and leaves denial fixtures alone. 02 no longer co-owns `test_guardian_parent_none.py`. |
| R5 tracer scope | MEDIUM | incorporated | 01 | Atomic-slice rationale written into 01-01 with staged assertions. Acceptable. |
| R6 parser vs deployment limits | LOW | incorporated | 01, 04 | Interface text and 04-02 separate library bounds from proxy/server request-line rejection. |
| R7 singular `cv_cancel_key=None` | LOW | incorporated | 04 | 04-01 documents current-view resolution (`base.py:501-502`) separately from plural static `None`. |

Remaining findings R8–R21 are classified in each plan section.

## 06.7-01

**Summary.** The revised plan now owns the whole blast radius of the default change. The dispatch-level cleanup is placed on the one seam every list and card view passes through, the four affected goldens are named exactly, and the twelve regression modules are in both `files_modified` and the wave-1 gate. The `"auto"` representation lands on `CrudViewProcessFormMixin`, which the MRO check confirms covers create (`create.py:12`), update (`update.py:9`), delete (`delete.py:51`), both custom forms (`form.py:10,40`), workflow (`crud_views_workflow/lib/views.py:18` via `CustomFormView`) and polymorphic create (`crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create.py:13` via `CreateView`), while `CrudView.cv_cancel_keys=None` (`base.py:82`) keeps list, card, detail and action views static. One genuine gap remains around custom origin keys.

**Strengths.**
- The interface replaces every raw iteration of `cv_cancel_keys` that would otherwise splat the string: `base.py:176` (E252), `:518-525`, `:745` and `:796`. Grok's cycle-1 foot-gun is addressed by `effective_cancel_keys()` as the single reader.
- The token binds `[cv_viewset.app or "", cv_viewset.name]`; `ViewSet.app` exists (`src/crud_views/lib/viewset/__init__.py:71`) and the list-to-card alias the plan relies on is at `:339-343`.
- Deterministic non-timestamp `Signer` under `SECRET_KEY="django-testing"` (`tests/test1/conftest.py:46`) makes the golden-normalization validation stable, and R11 fallback-key tests are specified in 01-03.
- `get_cancel_button` returning `Button | None` before customization, with `get_form_actions` and `CrispyDeleteForm.helper` (`src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py:57-58,138-149`) filtering `None`, avoids the URL-less button fallback at `src/crud_views/lib/view/button_data.py:354-357`.
- R17 is narrower than the existing create rendering adapter, which catches `(Http404, KeyError)` at `create.py:67`; the plan's seam catches only built-in `Http404`. That is the safer choice for a new contract and should not be "harmonized" during execution.

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM (new). Restore marker and dispatch cleanup are keyed to the literal keys `list`/`card`, but explicit `cv_cancel_keys` may name a custom key (D-03).** A second list view registered under another key (for example `archive`) listed as an origin receives the captured query (interface: "appended to an accepted origin only") but never the restore scalar, and its dispatch never consumes one. If that view composes `ListViewTableFilterMixin` with persistence on (the default, `settings.py:79`), an explicitly empty capture reaches the bare-list branch at `mixins.py:395-397` and revives the remembered filters. That is the exact D-06 failure, confined to custom-key origins. Key the restore append and the dispatch consumption on the destination's class family (a `ListView`/`CardListView` subclass, or `cv_object is False` on GET) rather than on the key string.
- **LOW.** Every row and toolbar link signs an identical payload per request. HMAC cost is small, but a request-local memo on the rendering view is a one-line win; not required.
- **LOW.** The captured query is also appended to accepted *detail* origins. Harmless, but 04-01 should say so, or the resolver should limit the append to list-family destinations alongside the restore marker.
- **LOW.** 01-01's verify runs the tracer serially with `-k`; later gates use bounded workers. Fine, noted only because the ledger claims "focused tests run serial only for the single tracer".

**Suggestions.**
- Define eligibility for restore and cleanup once, as a predicate on the destination class, and reuse it in `cv_get_cancel_url` and `_cv_consume_cancel_restore`.
- Add one 01-03 case with an explicit custom-key list origin and an empty capture under enabled persistence.

**Dispositions.** R2, R3, R4: FULLY RESOLVED. R5, R6, R11, R15, R17, R18, R19: incorporated as executable text. Nothing deferred.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** Design and ownership are now correct; the custom-key restore gap is bounded and cheap to close before execution.

## 06.7-02

**Summary.** E256 is free and the plan reserves it with a declaration-uniqueness test. The Guardian adapter now has explicit object, queryset and parent-only branches, which matters because the composite MROs place `GuardianQuerysetMixin` before `ListViewPermissionRequired` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:245-247`) and `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired.cv_has_access(user, None)` returns `False` (`views.py:381-382`); an unconditional `super()` chain would reintroduce the model-permission check the parent mixin deliberately bypasses (`mixins.py:262-265`). The registry inventory for R8 is executable.

**Strengths.**
- My own inventory of `tests/test1/app/views.py` agrees with the plan's expectation that app-level fixtures produce no E256: every ViewSet that registers a form also registers `list` or `card` (`author_detail_only`, `author_custom_detail` and `contract` carry no forms). The remaining exposure is module-local fixtures, which 02-01 covers with the collect-all run.
- Access-before-state ordering and the single state evaluation match `base.py:357-379` and the existing nested-create adapter at `create.py:51-70` (R15).
- Reusing `_guardian_user_has_parent_perm` (`mixins.py:302-323`) keeps the None-key and accept-global semantics `test_guardian_parent_none.py` already pins.
- R9 serialization is explicit: only 02 writes `base.py` in wave 2.

**Concerns.**
- **LOW (accepted design, flagging for the record).** Startup E256 is skipped whenever any eligible auto origin exists, so an explicitly overridden but unregistered `cv_cancel_key` on an auto form passes `manage.py check` and raises `ImproperlyConfigured` on the first direct visit without an origin. Gemini's alternative (always report an explicit override that is unregistered or object-typed on a no-object form) is statically detectable and cheap. Not a blocker; the runtime raise is tested.
- **LOW.** The first 02-01 verify uses a `-k` name filter; 02-02's full-module run is the backstop.
- **LOW.** Anonymous handling in the Guardian adapter is defensive since form pages deny anonymous users at dispatch (`mixins.py:270-271`); keep it, but the matrix should not pretend it is reachable through a rendered form.

**Suggestions.**
- Consider reporting an *explicitly set* invalid `cv_cancel_key` at startup regardless of auto origins; keep the deliberate lazy policy only for the inherited default.

**Dispositions.** R1: FULLY RESOLVED. R4 (ownership): FULLY RESOLVED. R8, R9, R15, R16, R17: incorporated. Nothing deferred.

**Risk: LOW.**

## 06.7-05

**Summary.** The verification design is sound and now consumes plan-01's golden and fixture policies instead of discovering them. The final gate list matches the research matrix, example tests and changed-example lint are included, and `node --test` follows the existing `tests/browser/presentation-config.test.cjs` precedent. The R21 fix, however, introduces a prerequisite the repository cannot satisfy on its own.

**Strengths.**
- Tab A/B isolation in one browser context is the right real-browser proof of D-06.
- The 6.0 floor run keeps only the documented deselect; no new exclusions.
- Human checks stay distinct from automation and the acceptance file records runtime identity.

**Concerns.**
- **MEDIUM (new, introduced by the R21 remedy). `CV_CANCEL_ASSETS_DIR/assets.json` has no provisioning path and the two tasks disagree on what its absence means.** The manifest exists nowhere in the repo (grep finds it only in this plan and VALIDATION.md). The fixture base loads five CDN assets (`tests/test1/app/templates/app/base.html:14-28`), and the accepted harness for this exact page fetches them live with SRI verification and a distinct `infrastructure_prerequisite_failed` exit code 2 (`tests/browser/presentation.cjs:47-55,65-117,442-444`). 05-01 says a missing directory leaves browser acceptance "unverified"; 05-02's verify says any skipped browser case fails the gate. As written, CAN-07 browser evidence depends on undocumented manual work. Either add a documented provisioning step (download the five declared URLs, verify their declared SRI, write `assets.json`) as a precondition script or task, or admit the `presentation.cjs` model (live CDN plus SRI plus recorded hashes, exit 2 when unreachable) as an accepted alternative path, and align 05-02's `fails_when` with 05-01's "unverified" outcome.
- **LOW.** Phase 06.6's companion harness has a `cancelNavigation` case (`tests/browser/theme-adoption.cjs:312-324`) that reads `data-cv-cancel-url` on `/create/`. D-13 may affect copied cancel templates in that companion. No plan re-runs it; the acceptance file should record "companion acceptance: not re-run" explicitly.

**Suggestions.**
- Adopt `presentation.cjs`'s exit-2 convention in `cancel-navigation.cjs` and make 05-02 treat exit 2 as "browser evidence unverified, phase not complete" rather than as a test failure.

**Dispositions.** R3, R4: FULLY RESOLVED (consumer side). R13, R21: incorporated, with the R21 remedy carrying the MEDIUM above. Nothing deferred.

**Risk: MEDIUM.** The harness is well designed; the asset prerequisite can block the only real-browser gate.

## Cross-plan assessment

**Round 1 HIGH findings.** R1, R2, R3, R4, R12, R13 and R16 are all FULLY RESOLVED at the plan level: each has a concrete owning task, a verify command and a stated failure condition, which is the standard for pre-execution plans. No HIGH remains open.

**Remaining findings.** R5–R9, R11, R14, R15, R17–R21 are incorporated in executable text. None is deferred or rejected; no ledger entry claims more than the plan delivers.

**Genuine new gaps, in priority order.**
1. Plan 01: restore and cleanup keyed to the `list`/`card` key literals, leaving explicit custom-key list origins without explicit-empty restoration (MEDIUM).
2. Plan 05: the local asset manifest prerequisite has no provisioning path and 05-01/05-02 disagree on absence semantics (MEDIUM).
3. Plan 02: startup E256 stays silent for an explicitly invalid `cv_cancel_key` when an auto origin exists (LOW, accepted design).
4. Plan 03: the selector's own Cancel is a test-app control, not a stock one (LOW, wording).

**Dependency ordering.** Wave order 01 → (02 ∥ 03) → 04 → 05 is correct for the declared files, and the R9 serialization rule closes the only non-file coupling. Wave-2 file overlap is zero.

**Scope and security.** No scope creep. The signed transport remains integrity-only, destinations are always reversed from the current ViewSet and parent kwargs, and the three distinct outcomes (invalid input, inaccessible destination, developer error) are each tested. The threat registers trace to real seams.

**Phase goals.** The five plans cover CAN-01 through CAN-07 and D-01 through D-16 with owning tasks. The two MEDIUM items above do not change that coverage; they affect one edge of D-06 and the executability of the browser gate.

**Overall risk: MEDIUM**, down from HIGH in cycle 1. Both remaining MEDIUM items are small text changes to plans 01 and 05 and should be made before execution starts.

## OpenCode Review (gemini-review) — cycle 2

## 06.7-01

### Summary
Revised Plan `06.7-01` (`dfd98c2`) closes the Cycle 1 architectural and test-ownership gaps across `src/crud_views/lib/view/cancel.py`, `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py`, `src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py`, and `src/crud_views/lib/crispy/form.py`. In particular, it moves `cv_from_restore=1` consumption and navigation-parameter stripping to a shared `CrudView._cv_consume_cancel_restore()` hook invoked from `CrudView.dispatch()` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:227`) before `super().dispatch()` for all GET list/card views (R2), preserves the existing target rendering-state hook `cls._cv_action_enabled_for_rendering(user, action_obj, rendering_view)` (`base.py:377`) with narrow `Http404` handling around built-in parent resolution (R15, R17), introduces `encode_cancel_state` and the bounded `"invalid"` token marker to prevent invalid captures from degrading into legacy key-only origins (R18, R19), and takes explicit Wave 1 ownership of the 4 action-menu golden comparisons and 12 regression modules (R3, R4). Only minor low-severity implementation details remain to watch during execution.

### Strengths
- **Shared dispatch-boundary restoration cleanup (R2)**: Calling `_cv_consume_cancel_restore()` from `CrudView.dispatch()` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:227`) before `super().dispatch()` ensures that plain `ListView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/list.py:19`), `CardListView` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/card.py:17`), and `ListViewTableFilterMixin` (`src/crud_views/lib/views/mixins.py:357`) all strip `cv_from`, `cv_from_cancel`, and `cv_from_restore` from both `request.GET` and `request.META["QUERY_STRING"]` before `CardOrderMixin.get_context_data()` (`mixins.py:262-280`), `cv_pagination` (`src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:558-569`), `cv_querystring` (`crud_views.py:301-309`), or sibling link emission runs.
- **Explicit Wave 1 ownership of golden normalization and affected fixture migrations (R3, R4)**: Task `01-02` (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:113,125-129`) owns and verifies all 12 affected test modules (`test_action_menus_golden.py`, `test_action_menus_list.py`, `test_guardian_parent_none.py`, `test_authorization_templates.py`, `test_authorization_matrix.py`, `test_sibling_context_button.py`, `test_core_nested_create_state.py`, `test_inaccessible_actions_resolve.py`, `test_action_menus_groups.py`, `test_theme_form_requests.py`, `test_form_buttons_golden.py`, `test_polymorphic.py`), validating emitted route/origin/token values before narrowly stripping only navigation parameters for the four action-menu golden comparisons and adding local `view` grants where Cancel presence is part of the fixture contract.
- **Tri-state capture representation (`query=""` vs `query=None` vs `"invalid"` marker, R18/R19)**: `CancelCapture(origin, query)` cleanly separates captured empty query state (`query=""`, which emits `cv_from_restore=1` and clears remembered session filters) from legacy key-only origins (`query=None`, which adds neither query nor restore marker) and present-but-invalid tokens (`"invalid"` marker, which forces fallback even when `cv_from` is retained for `cv_success_keys`).
- **Preservation of target `_cv_action_enabled_for_rendering` and narrow `Http404` parent handling (R15, R17)**: `<interfaces>` (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:102`) delegates state checks to `cls._cv_action_enabled_for_rendering(user, action_obj, rendering_view)` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:377`, `src/crud_views/lib/views/create.py:52`) after `cls.cv_has_access(user, target_obj)`, and catches `Http404` only around built-in `rendering_view.cv_get_parent_object()` (`base.py:864-876`) so missing kwargs `KeyError` and custom hook exceptions still propagate.

### Concerns
- **[LOW] Required property — `clean_navigation_query()` / `capture_cancel_state()` must tolerate synthetic `SimpleNamespace(GET={})` requests used in unit tests (CAN-02, R4)**:
  - **Evidence**: `tests/test1/test_core_nested_create_state.py:97-105` and `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:560-563`.
  - **Mechanism**: `tests/test1/test_core_nested_create_state.py` (now in `06.7-01-PLAN.md`'s `files_modified` at line 21) constructs a synthetic request via `_view()` at line 103: `view.request = SimpleNamespace(user=AnonymousUser(), GET={}, resolver_match=SimpleNamespace(url_name="test"))`, where `request.GET` is a plain `dict` rather than a `QueryDict`. When `test_renamed_create_button_uses_url_parent` (`line 89`) or `test_create_rendering_entry_points_keep_parent_and_adapter` (`line 137`) calls `cv_get_link_url()` on `BookCreateView` (`cv_cancel_keys = "auto"`), `capture_cancel_state()` will clean `request.GET` and serialize it. A plain `dict` has `.copy()` and `.pop()`, but not `.urlencode()`. Unless `clean_navigation_query()` / `capture_cancel_state()` uses `cleaned.urlencode() if hasattr(cleaned, "urlencode") else urlencode(cleaned, doseq=True)` (matching `cv_pagination` at `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:563`), calling `.urlencode()` on `request.GET` in `_view()` will raise `AttributeError: 'dict' object has no attribute 'urlencode'`.
- **[LOW] Required property — `cv_get_cancel_key()` and `cv_get_cancel_url()` must read token-only selector captures when `cv_from` is absent from `request.GET` (CAN-01, CAN-04, R19)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:733-772` and `06.7-01-PLAN.md:100,106`.
  - **Mechanism**: `<interfaces>` (`06.7-01-PLAN.md:100`) specifies that *"a missing shared scalar is valid for a signed cancel-only selector handoff, whose origin comes from the payload"*. Currently, `cv_get_cancel_key()` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:760`) calls `self.cv_get_allowed_origin_cls(self.cv_cancel_keys)`, which calls `self.cv_get_origin_key()` (`base.py:733-738`) and returns `None` when `cv_from` is absent from `request.GET`. Both `cv_get_cancel_url()` and `cv_get_cancel_key()` must consult `read_cancel_state(self)` before falling back to `cv_get_origin_key()` so that token-only selector redirects resolve the origin key from `payload.origin` while `cv_get_success_key()` (`base.py:774-788`) continues reading `cv_from` only.

### Suggestions
- In `src/crud_views/lib/view/cancel.py`, serialize cleaned query mappings with `cleaned.urlencode() if hasattr(cleaned, "urlencode") else urlencode(cleaned, doseq=True)` so synthetic `dict`-backed `request.GET` objects in unit tests work without `AttributeError`.
- Ensure both `cv_get_cancel_url()` and `cv_get_cancel_key()` resolve a validated `CancelCapture.origin` from `read_cancel_state(self)` even when `cv_from` is omitted on a selector-forwarded request, while immediately rejecting `cv_from` when `cv_from_cancel` is present and invalid.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW** — All Cycle 1 HIGH and MEDIUM concerns on Plan `06.7-01` (R2, R3, R4, R5, R17, R18, R19) have concrete, verifiable tasks and acceptance criteria in the revised plan.

## 06.7-02

### Summary
Revised Plan `06.7-02` (`dfd98c2`) resolves all Cycle 1 blockers in `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py`, and `tests/test1/test_cancel_permissions.py`. It replaces the colliding `viewset.E255` identifier with `viewset.E256` and adds a source-declaration uniqueness test alongside existing `test_context_button_check.py` (R1), adds `test_registered_cancel_fallback_inventory` across both normal app imports and full suite collection to audit all registered cancel-owning forms (R8), defines explicit Guardian target-availability branches so nested parent-only/no-object targets (`GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired` and custom parent-only views) use their parent permission and `cv_create_has_access` without falling through to model-level `cv_has_access(user, None)` or double-calling `cv_action_enabled` (R16), and establishes sole Wave 2 write ownership over `src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py` (R9).

### Strengths
- **Unique `viewset.E256` diagnostic with source-wide uniqueness verification (R1)**: Task `02-01` (`06.7-02-PLAN.md:62-64`) assigns `viewset.E256` in `CrudView.checks` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:141`), preserves `viewset.E255` in `src/crud_views/lib/view/buttons.py:392`, and verifies uniqueness via `test_fallback_diagnostic_identifier_is_unique` and `tests/test1/test_context_button_check.py`.
- **Complete startup registry inventory (R8)**: `test_registered_cancel_fallback_inventory` (`06.7-02-PLAN.md:63-64`) enumerates every cancel-owning form in the imported ViewSet registry (`src/crud_views/lib/viewset/__init__.py:43`) and under full test collection (`pytest tests/test1 -k registered_cancel_fallback_inventory -q`), isolating any intentional invalid test fixtures with a restored registry snapshot while keeping `E256` at Error severity per D-09.
- **Explicit Guardian target branches matching request gates (R16)**: Task `02-02` (`06.7-02-PLAN.md:71-72`) avoids unconditional `super()` fallthrough to `CrudViewPermissionRequiredMixin.cv_has_access` (`src/crud_views/lib/view/base.py:926-930`). It validates `_guardian_child_composition_error()` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:235`), checks authenticated and active user status (`mixins.py:14-21,46,119,270`), evaluates `_guardian_user_has_parent_perm(user, parent_obj)` (`mixins.py:303`) first for nested targets, branches explicitly across object targets (`GuardianObjectPermissionMixin`), queryset targets (`GuardianQuerysetMixin`), and nested parent-only/create targets (`cv_create_has_access` at `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:310`), and invokes `_cv_action_enabled_for_rendering` exactly once.

### Concerns
- **[LOW] Required property — `_cv_cancel_target_available` on top-level `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired` must still deny inactive superusers under `AllowAllUsersModelBackend` (CAN-05, D-10, D-15)**:
  - **Evidence**: `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:257,378-380`, `src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:272-273`, and `tests/test1/test_guardian_policy.py:22-23`.
  - **Mechanism**: For a top-level `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired` (`cls.cv_viewset.parent is None`), `GuardianCreateViewPermissionRequired.cv_has_access(user, obj)` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/views.py:378-379`) delegates to `super().cv_has_access(user, obj)` (`CrudViewPermissionRequiredMixin.cv_has_access`), which calls `user.has_perms(perms)` without checking `_guardian_inactive_actor(user)`. Meanwhile, `GuardianParentPermissionMixin.dispatch()` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:272-273`) denies `_guardian_inactive_actor(request.user)` before checking `self.cv_viewset.parent`. Running the authenticated + `not _guardian_inactive_actor(user)` guard at the top of `GuardianParentPermissionMixin._cv_cancel_target_available` (before checking `cls.cv_viewset.parent`) ensures top-level Guardian views also deny inactive superusers when `ALLOW_INACTIVE` is active.

### Suggestions
- In `GuardianParentPermissionMixin._cv_cancel_target_available`, check `if not getattr(user, "is_authenticated", False) or _guardian_inactive_actor(user): return False` before branching on `cls.cv_viewset.parent is not None` so top-level and nested Guardian views share the same inactive-actor and anonymous-user gate as `GuardianParentPermissionMixin.dispatch()` (`src/crud_views_guardian/lib/mixins.py:270-273`).

### Risk Assessment
**LOW** — All Cycle 1 HIGH, MEDIUM, and LOW findings on Plan `06.7-02` (R1, R4, R8, R9, R15, R16, R17) are thoroughly addressed with concrete acceptance criteria and verification commands.
