## 06.7-03

### Summary
Revised Plan `06.7-03` (`dfd98c2`) cleanly resolves all Cycle 1 findings on `src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py` and `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py`. By forwarding only the bounded `cv_from_cancel` token (with `payload.origin` supplying the cancel origin when `cv_from` is absent, `encode_cancel_state(view, origin, None)` handling validated legacy key-only origins, and `"invalid"` preserving rejected token state), `PolymorphicCreateSelectView.form_valid()` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:68-72`) preserves cancel origin state across type selection without altering `cv_success_keys` behavior (R18, R19). Task `03-02` also normalizes empty/unresolved template `obj` arguments in `cv_cancel_url` (R20), tests custom `get_form_actions` and delete-helper subclasses filtering `None` (R14), and sequences any core `base.py` defect through Plan `06.7-02` (R9), while Plan `06.7-01` already migrates `tests/test1/test_polymorphic.py` in Wave 1 (R4).

### Strengths
- **Token-only selector forwarding preserves existing `cv_success_keys` behavior (R18, R19)**: Because `PolymorphicCreateSelectView.form_valid()` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:68-72`) currently drops `cv_from`, forwarding only `cv_from_cancel` (whose signed payload already binds `origin`, `viewset`, and `parents`, or carries `"invalid"` when rejected) ensures concrete `PolymorphicCreateView` (`src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create.py:13`) restores the original list/card Cancel destination without inadvertently activating dynamic save redirects that the selector previously dropped.
- **Complete 4-axis selector × concrete configuration matrix (R19)**: Task `03-01` (`06.7-03-PLAN.md:64`) tests `selector cancel (auto/None) × concrete cancel (auto/None) × selector success (None/allowed-list) × concrete success (None/allowed-list)`, covering `static → auto` legacy `query=null` minting when `cv_from` was emitted for `selector.cv_success_keys`.
- **Template tag `obj` normalization and custom `FormActions` subclass coverage (R14, R20)**: Task `03-02` (`06.7-03-PLAN.md:73`) normalizes `obj = obj if obj else None` in `cv_cancel_url` (matching `cv_context_url` at `src/crud_views/templatetags/crud_views.py:284`) and tests custom `get_form_actions()` and `CrispyDeleteForm` helper subclasses filtering `None`.

### Concerns
- **[LOW] Required property — `PolymorphicCreateSelectView.form_valid()` must check `read_cancel_state(self)` against the concrete `create` view's allowed cancel origins rather than the selector's own `cv_cancel_keys` when `static → auto` or `auto → custom-list` is configured (CAN-04, R19)**:
  - **Evidence**: `06.7-03-PLAN.md:63-64` and `src/crud_views_polymorphic/lib/create_select.py:68-72`.
  - **Mechanism**: Task `03-01` states: *"Validate with `read_cancel_state`, then require the concrete create class's effective allowed origin and no-object eligibility. For absent-token legacy input, validate the original key against registered same-ViewSet no-object eligibility and concrete allowed keys before minting a bounded token through NEW `encode_cancel_state(view, origin, None)`"*. If `read_cancel_state(self)` internally filters against `effective_cancel_keys(self)` (the selector's own `cv_cancel_keys`), then in the `static → auto` case (where `selector.cv_cancel_keys = None` and `concrete.cv_cancel_keys = "auto"`), `read_cancel_state(self)` on the selector would see `effective_cancel_keys(selector) == ()` unless `read_cancel_state` validates the token's cryptographic/ViewSet/parent envelope independently of the caller's allow-list (or accepts an explicit `owner`/`allowed_keys` parameter).

### Suggestions
- Ensure `read_cancel_state(view)` in `src/crud_views/lib/view/cancel.py` validates the token's signature, schema, ViewSet, parent coordinates, and `cv_from` match without requiring `origin in effective_cancel_keys(view)` inside `read_cancel_state` itself (or allow passing `allowed_keys=effective_cancel_keys(create_cls)`), so `PolymorphicCreateSelectView.form_valid()` can validate the token against the concrete `create` view's `effective_cancel_keys`.

### Risk Assessment
**LOW** — All Cycle 1 concerns on Plan `06.7-03` (R4, R9, R14, R18, R19, R20) are resolved in the revised plan.

When Task `04-03` updates `AuthorUpdateView` in `examples/bootstrap5/library/views.py` and `examples/bootstrap5/project/features.py:81-82`, removing the redundant `cv_cancel_keys = ["list", "detail"]` on `AuthorDeleteView` (`library/views.py:106`) as well keeps both views consistent with the updated `features.py` text while preserving the contiguous match for `tutorial-3-forms.md:76-83`.
